def _source_audit_edit(edge):
    """Do not represent generated framework guesses as remembered facts."""
    evidence = edge.get('evidence') or ''
    if 'FW-MEMORY' in evidence:
        edge['evidence'] = evidence.replace('FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED')

    # Exact source predicates/texts replace generator prose gates. The kind
    # maps below classify these exact strings after this edit runs.
    if edge.get('src') == 'S_ENTRY' and edge.get('dst') == 'S_SYS_BootReceiver':
        edge['env'] = 'isBootCompleted(action)'
    elif edge.get('src') == 'S_ENTRY' and edge.get('dst') in {
        'MainActivity.onCreate', 'MainActivity.<init>',
        'SendTextToClipboard.<init>', 'SendTextToClipboard.onCreate',
        'AddFeedActivity.<init>', 'AddFeedActivity.onCreate',
        'AddTorrentActivity.<init>', 'AddTorrentActivity.onCreate',
        'SettingsActivity.<init>', 'SettingsActivity.onCreate'
    }:
        edge['env'] = 'android:exported="true"'
    elif edge.get('src') == 'TorrentNotifier.makeTorrentFinishedNotify' and edge.get('dst') == 'DetailTorrentActivity.<init>':
        edge['env'] = 'openPendingIntent'
    elif edge.get('src') == 'TorrentService.initPendingIntents' and edge.get('dst') == 'S_SYS_NotificationReceiver':
        edge['env'] = 'PendingIntent.getBroadcast('
    elif edge.get('src') == 'Scheduler.setStartStopAppAlarm' and edge.get('dst') == 'S_SYS_SchedulerReceiver':
        edge['env'] = 'calendar.getTimeInMillis()'

    if edge.get('needs_state') == 'The list / adapter shows at least one row (some user data exists: add an item first)':
        edge['needs_state'] = 'getAdapter().getItemCount()'

/*
 * The server that allows to stream selected file from a torrent and to which a specific address is assigned.
 * Supports partial content and DLNA (for some file formats)
 */

public class TorrentStreamServer extends NanoHTTPD
{
    private static final String TAG = TorrentStreamServer.class.getSimpleName();

    private static final String MIME_OCTET_STREAM = "application/octet-stream";

    private static HashMap<String, DLNAFileType> DLNA_FILE_TYPES;
    static {
        DLNA_FILE_TYPES = new HashMap<>();
        DLNA_FILE_TYPES.put("mp4", new DLNAFileType("mp4", "video/mp4", "DLNA.ORG_PN=AVC_MP4_BL_L3L_SD_AAC;DLNA.ORG_OP=01;DLNA.ORG_CI=0;DLNA.ORG_FLAGS=01700000000000000000000000000000", "Streaming"));
        DLNA_FILE_TYPES.put("avi", new DLNAFileType("avi", "video/x-msvideo", "DLNA.ORG_PN=AVC_MP4_BL_L3L_SD_AAC;DLNA.ORG_OP=01;DLNA.ORG_CI=0;DLNA.ORG_FLAGS=01700000000000000000000000000000", "Streaming"));
        DLNA_FILE_TYPES.put("mkv", new DLNAFileType("mkv", "video/x-matroska", "DLNA.ORG_PN=AVC_MKV_MP_HD_AC3;DLNA.ORG_OP=01;DLNA.ORG_CI=0;DLNA.ORG_FLAGS=01700000000000000000000000000000", "Streaming"));
    }

    private TorrentEngine engine;

    public TorrentStreamServer(@NonNull String host, int port)
    {
        super(host, port);
    }

    public void start(@NonNull Context appContext) throws IOException
    {
        Log.i(TAG, "Start " + TAG);

        engine = TorrentEngine.getInstance(appContext);

        super.start();
    }

    @Override
    public void stop()
    {
        super.stop();

        Log.i(TAG, "Stop " + TAG);
    }

    /*
     * URL format: http://'hostname':'port'/stream?file='file_index'&torrent='torrent_hash'
     */

    public static String makeStreamUrl(@NonNull String hostname, int port,
                                       @NonNull String torrentId, int fileIndex)
    {
        try {
            return new URI("http", null, hostname, port,
                           "/stream",
                           String.format(Locale.getDefault(),
                                         "file=%d&torrent=%s",
                                         fileIndex,
                                         torrentId),
                           null)
                          .toString();
        } catch (URISyntaxException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Response serve(IHTTPSession session)
    {
        String uri = session.getUri();
        String extension = uri.substring(uri.lastIndexOf('.') + 1);
        DLNAFileType fileType = DLNA_FILE_TYPES.get(extension);

        Response res = handleTorrent(session);
        if (fileType != null)
            fileType.setHeaders(res);

        return res;
    }

    public Response handleTorrent(IHTTPSession httpSession)
    {
        if (engine == null)
            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");

        if (!httpSession.getUri().equals("/stream"))
            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");

        Map<String, String> params = httpSession.getParms();
        if (params.size() < 2)
            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");

        String torrentId = Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("torrent"));

        int fileIndex;
        TorrentStream stream;
        try {

195	                     */
196	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
197	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
198	
199	                } else {
200	                    if (endAt < 0)

202	                    long newLen = endAt - startFrom + 1;
203	                    if (newLen < 0)

210	                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");

212	                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes " + startFrom + "-" + endAt + "/" + stream.fileSize);
213	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
214	                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
215	                }
216	            } else {
217	                if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null && startFrom >= stream.fileSize) {
218	                    /*
219	                     * Return the size of the file
220	                     * 4xx responses are not trumped by if-none-match
221	                     */
222	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "");
223	                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes */" + stream.fileSize);
224	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
225	
226	                } else if (range == null && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
227	                    /*
228	                     * Full-file-fetch request would return entire file
229	                     * respond with not-modified
230	                     */
231	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
232	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
233	
234	                } else if (!headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
235	                    /*
236	                     * Range request that doesn't match current etag
237	                     * would return entire (different) file respond with not-modified
238	                     */
239	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
240	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
241	
242	                } else {
243	                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
244	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(OK, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, stream.fileSize);
245	                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
246	                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + stream.fileSize);
247	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
248	                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
249	                }
250	            }
251	
252	            return res;
253	        } catch (Throwable e) {
254	            Log.e(TAG, Log.getStackTraceString(e));
255	
256	            return newFixedLengthResponse(FORBIDDEN, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "Forbidden");
257	        }

1053	        if (pref.watchDir())
1054	            startWatchDir();
1055	
1056	        boolean enableStreaming = pref.enableStreaming();
1057	        if (enableStreaming)
1058	            startStreamingServer();
1059	
1060	        loadTorrents();
1061	    }
1062	
1063	    private void startStreamingServer() {
1064	        stopStreamingServer();
1065	
1066	        String hostname = pref.streamingHostname();
1067	        int port = pref.streamingPort();
1068	
1069	        torrentStreamServer = new TorrentStreamServer(hostname, port);
1070	        try {
1071	            torrentStreamServer.start(appContext);
1072	
1073	        } catch (IOException e) {
1074	            Log.e(TAG, Log.getStackTraceString(e));
1075	            notifier.makeErrorNotify(appContext.getString(R.string.pref_streaming_error));
1076	        }
1077	    }
1078	
1079	    private void stopStreamingServer() {
1080	        if (torrentStreamServer != null)
1081	            torrentStreamServer.stop();
1082	        torrentStreamServer = null;
1083	    }
1084	
1085	    private void loadTorrents() {
1086	        disposables.add(Completable.fromRunnable(() -> {
1087	                    if (isRunning())
1088	                        session.restoreTorrents();
1089	
1090	                }).subscribeOn(Schedulers.io())
1091	                .subscribe());
1092	    }
1093	
1094	    private void setProxy() {
1095	        SessionSettings s = session.getSettings();
1096	
1097	        s.proxyType = SessionSettings.ProxyType.fromValue(pref.proxyType());
1098	        s.proxyAddress = pref.proxyAddress();
1099	        s.proxyPort = pref.proxyPort();
1100	        s.proxyPeersToo = pref.proxyPeersToo();
1101	        s.proxyRequiresAuth = pref.proxyRequiresAuth();
1102	        s.proxyLogin = pref.proxyLogin();
1103	        s.proxyPassword = pref.proxyPassword();
1104	
1105	        session.setSettings(s);
1106	    }
1107	
1108	    private SessionSettings.EncryptMode getEncryptInConnectionsMode() {
1109	        return SessionSettings.EncryptMode.fromValue(pref.encryptInConnectionsMode());
1110	    }

93	/**
94	 * A simple, tiny, nicely embeddable HTTP server in Java
95	 * <p/>
96	 * <p/>
97	 * NanoHTTPD
98	 * <p>
99	 * Copyright (c) 2012-2013 by Paul S. Hawke, 2001,2005-2013 by Jarno Elonen,
100	 * 2010 by Konstantinos Togias
101	 * </p>
102	 * <p/>
103	 * <p/>
104	 * <b>Features + limitations: </b>
105	 * <ul>
106	 * <p/>
107	 * <li>Only one Java file</li>
108	 * <li>Java 5 compatible</li>
109	 * <li>Released as open source, Modified BSD licence</li>
110	 * <li>No fixed config files, logging, authorization etc. (Implement yourself if
111	 * you need them.)</li>
112	 * <li>Supports parameter parsing of GET and POST methods (+ rudimentary PUT
113	 * support in 1.25)</li>
114	 * <li>Supports both dynamic content and file serving</li>
115	 * <li>Supports file upload (since version 1.2, 2010)</li>
116	 * <li>Supports partial content (streaming)</li>
117	 * <li>Supports ETags</li>
118	 * <li>Never caches anything</li>
119	 * <li>Doesn't limit bandwidth, request time or simultaneous connections</li>
120	 * <li>Default code serves files and shows all HTTP parameters and headers</li>
121	 * <li>File server supports directory listing, index.html and index.htm</li>
122	 * <li>File server supports partial content (streaming)</li>
123	 * <li>File server supports ETags</li>
124	 * <li>File server does the 301 redirection trick for directories without '/'</li>
125	 * <li>File server supports simple skipping for files (continue download)</li>
126	 * <li>File server serves also very long files without memory overhead</li>
127	 * <li>Contains a built-in list of most common MIME types</li>
128	 * <li>All header names are converted to lower case so they don't vary between
129	 * browsers/clients</li>
130	 * <p/>
131	 * </ul>
132	 * <p/>
133	 * <p/>
134	 * <b>How to use: </b>
135	 * <ul>
136	 * <p/>
137	 * <li>Subclass and implement serve() and embed to your own program</li>
138	 * <p/>
139	 * </ul>
140	 * <p/>
141	 * See the separate "LICENSE.md" file for the distribution license (Modified BSD
142	 * licence)
143	 */
144	public abstract class NanoHTTPD {
145	    /**
146	     * Pluggable strategy for asynchronously executing requests.
147	     */
148	    public interface AsyncRunner {
149	        void closeAll();
150	        void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler);
151	        void exec(ClientHandler code);
152	    }
153	    /**
154	     * The runnable that will be used for every new client connection.
155	     */
156	    public class ClientHandler implements Runnable {
157	        private final InputStream inputStream;
158	        private final Socket acceptSocket;
159	        private ClientHandler(InputStream inputStream, Socket acceptSocket) {
160	            this.inputStream = inputStream;
161	            this.acceptSocket = acceptSocket;
162	        }
163	        public void close() {
164	            safeClose(this.inputStream);
165	            safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
166	        }
167	        @Override
168	        public void run() {
169	            OutputStream outputStream = null;
170	            try {
171	                outputStream = this.acceptSocket.getOutputStream();
172	                TempFileManager tempFileManager = NanoHTTPD.this.tempFileManagerFactory.create();
173	                HTTPSession session = new HTTPSession(tempFileManager, this.inputStream, outputStream, this.acceptSocket.getInetAddress());
174	                while (!this.acceptSocket.isClosed()) {
175	                    session.execute();
176	                }
177	            } catch (Exception e) {
178	                // When the socket is closed by the client,
179	                // we throw our own SocketException
180	                // to break the "keep alive" loop above. If
181	                // the exception was anything other
182	                // than the expected SocketException OR a
183	                // SocketTimeoutException, print the
184	                // stacktrace
185	                if (!(e instanceof SocketException && "NanoHttpd Shutdown".equals(e.getMessage())) && !(e instanceof SocketTimeoutException)) {
186	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
187	                }
188	            } finally {
189	                safeClose(outputStream);
190	                safeClose(this.inputStream);
191	                safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
192	                NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.closed(this);
193	            }
194	        }
195	    }
196	    public static class Cookie {
197	        public static String getHTTPTime(int days) {
198	            Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance();
199	            SimpleDateFormat dateFormat = new SimpleDateFormat("EEE, dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss z", Locale.US);
200	            dateFormat.setTimeZone(TimeZone.getTimeZone("GMT"));
201	            calendar.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, days);
202	            return dateFormat.format(calendar.getTime());
203	        }
204	        private final String n, v, e;
205	        public Cookie(String name, String value) {
206	            this(name, value, 30);
207	        }
208	        public Cookie(String name, String value, int numDays) {
209	            this.n = name;
210	            this.v = value;
211	            this.e = getHTTPTime(numDays);
212	        }
213	        public Cookie(String name, String value, String expires) {
214	            this.n = name;
215	            this.v = value;
216	            this.e = expires;
217	        }
218	        public String getHTTPHeader() {
219	            String fmt = "%s=%s; expires=%s";
220	            return String.format(fmt, this.n, this.v, this.e);
221	        }
222	    }
223	    /**
224	     * Provides rudimentary support for cookies. Doesn't support 'path',
225	     * 'secure' nor 'httpOnly'. Feel free to improve it and/or add unsupported
226	     * features.
227	     *
228	     * @author LordFokas
229	     */
230	    public class CookieHandler implements Iterable<String> {
231	        private final HashMap<String, String> cookies = new HashMap<String, String>();
232	        private final ArrayList<Cookie> queue = new ArrayList<Cookie>();
233	        public CookieHandler(Map<String, String> httpHeaders) {
234	            String raw = httpHeaders.get("cookie");
235	            if (raw != null) {
236	                String[] tokens = raw.split(";");
237	                for (String token : tokens) {
238	                    String[] data = token.trim().split("=");
239	                    if (data.length == 2) {
240	                        this.cookies.put(data[0], data[1]);
241	                    }
242	                }
243	            }
244	        }
245	        /**
246	         * Set a cookie with an expiration date from a month ago, effectively
247	         * deleting it on the client side.
248	         *
249	         * @param name
250	         *            The cookie name.
251	         */
252	        public void delete(String name) {
253	            set(name, "-delete-", -30);
254	        }
255	        @NonNull
256	        @Override
257	        public Iterator<String> iterator() {
258	            return this.cookies.keySet().iterator();
259	        }
260	        /**
### CMD: awk NR>=261 && NR<=520 NanoHTTPD.java (numbered)
261	         * Read a cookie from the HTTP Headers.
262	         *
263	         * @param name
264	         *            The cookie's name.
265	         * @return The cookie's value if it exists, null otherwise.
266	         */
267	        public String read(String name) {
268	            return this.cookies.get(name);
269	        }
270	        public void set(Cookie cookie) {
271	            this.queue.add(cookie);
272	        }
273	        /**
274	         * Sets a cookie.
275	         *
276	         * @param name
277	         *            The cookie's name.
278	         * @param value
279	         *            The cookie's value.
280	         * @param expires
281	         *            How many days until the cookie expires.
282	         */
283	        public void set(String name, String value, int expires) {
284	            this.queue.add(new Cookie(name, value, Cookie.getHTTPTime(expires)));
285	        }
286	        /**
287	         * Internally used by the webserver to add all queued cookies into the
288	         * Response's HTTP Headers.
289	         *
290	         * @param response
291	         *            The Response object to which headers the queued cookies
292	         *            will be added.
293	         */
294	        public void unloadQueue(Response response) {
295	            for (Cookie cookie : this.queue) {
296	                response.addHeader("Set-Cookie", cookie.getHTTPHeader());
297	            }
298	        }
299	    }
300	    /**
301	     * Default threading strategy for NanoHTTPD.
302	     * <p/>
303	     * <p>
304	     * By default, the server spawns a new Thread for every incoming request.
305	     * These are set to <i>daemon</i> status, and named according to the request
306	     * number. The name is useful when profiling the application.
307	     * </p>
308	     */
309	    public static class DefaultAsyncRunner implements AsyncRunner {
310	        private long requestCount;
311	        private final List<ClientHandler> running = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<NanoHTTPD.ClientHandler>());
312	        /**
313	         * @return a list with currently running clients.
314	         */
315	        public List<ClientHandler> getRunning() {
316	            return running;
317	        }
318	        @Override
319	        public void closeAll() {
320	            // copy of the list for concurrency
321	            for (ClientHandler clientHandler : new ArrayList<ClientHandler>(this.running)) {
322	                clientHandler.close();
323	            }
324	        }
325	        @Override
326	        public void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
327	            this.running.remove(clientHandler);
328	        }
329	        @Override
330	        public void exec(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
331	            ++this.requestCount;
332	            Thread t = new Thread(clientHandler);
333	            t.setDaemon(true);
334	            t.setName("NanoHttpd Request Processor (#" + this.requestCount + ")");
335	            this.running.add(clientHandler);
336	            t.start();
337	        }
338	    }
339	    /**
340	     * Default strategy for creating and cleaning up temporary files.
341	     * <p/>
342	     * <p>
343	     * By default, files are created by <code>File.createTempFile()</code> in
344	     * the directory specified.
345	     * </p>
346	     */
347	    public static class DefaultTempFile implements TempFile {
348	        private final File file;
349	        private final OutputStream fstream;
350	        public DefaultTempFile(File tempdir) throws IOException {
351	            this.file = File.createTempFile("NanoHTTPD-", "", tempdir);
352	            this.fstream = new FileOutputStream(this.file);
353	        }
354	        @Override
355	        public void delete() throws Exception {
356	            safeClose(this.fstream);
357	            if (!this.file.delete()) {
358	                throw new Exception("could not delete temporary file");
359	            }
360	        }
361	        @Override
362	        public String getName() {
363	            return this.file.getAbsolutePath();
364	        }
365	        @Override
366	        public OutputStream open() throws Exception {
367	            return this.fstream;
368	        }
369	    }
370	    /**
371	     * Default strategy for creating and cleaning up temporary files.
372	     * <p/>
373	     * <p>
374	     * This class stores its files in the standard location (that is, wherever
375	     * <code>java.io.tmpdir</code> points to). Files are added to an internal
376	     * list, and deleted when no longer needed (that is, when
377	     * <code>clear()</code> is invoked at the end of processing a request).
378	     * </p>
379	     */
380	    public static class DefaultTempFileManager implements TempFileManager {
381	        private final File tmpdir;
382	        private final List<TempFile> tempFiles;
383	        public DefaultTempFileManager() {
384	            this.tmpdir = new File(System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir"));
385	            if (!tmpdir.exists()) {
386	                tmpdir.mkdirs();
387	            }
388	            this.tempFiles = new ArrayList<TempFile>();
389	        }
390	        @Override
391	        public void clear() {
392	            for (TempFile file : this.tempFiles) {
393	                try {
394	                    file.delete();
395	                } catch (Exception ignored) {
396	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "could not delete file ", ignored);
397	                }
398	            }
399	            this.tempFiles.clear();
400	        }
401	        @Override
402	        public TempFile createTempFile(String filename_hint) throws Exception {
403	            DefaultTempFile tempFile = new DefaultTempFile(this.tmpdir);
404	            this.tempFiles.add(tempFile);
405	            return tempFile;
406	        }
407	    }
408	    /**
409	     * Default strategy for creating and cleaning up temporary files.
410	     */
411	    private class DefaultTempFileManagerFactory implements TempFileManagerFactory {
412	        @Override
413	        public TempFileManager create() {
414	            return new DefaultTempFileManager();
415	        }
416	    }

431	     * Creates a new SSLServerSocket
432	     */
433	    public static class SecureServerSocketFactory implements ServerSocketFactory {
434	        private SSLServerSocketFactory sslServerSocketFactory;
435	        private String[] sslProtocols;
436	        public SecureServerSocketFactory(SSLServerSocketFactory sslServerSocketFactory, String[] sslProtocols) {
437	            this.sslServerSocketFactory = sslServerSocketFactory;
438	            this.sslProtocols = sslProtocols;
439	        }
440	        @Override
441	        public ServerSocket create() throws IOException {
442	            SSLServerSocket ss = null;
443	            ss = (SSLServerSocket) this.sslServerSocketFactory.createServerSocket();
444	            if (this.sslProtocols != null) {
445	                ss.setEnabledProtocols(this.sslProtocols);
446	            } else {
447	                ss.setEnabledProtocols(ss.getSupportedProtocols());
448	            }
449	            ss.setUseClientMode(false);
450	            ss.setWantClientAuth(false);
451	            ss.setNeedClientAuth(false);
452	            return ss;
453	        }
454	    }
455	    private static final String CONTENT_DISPOSITION_REGEX = "([ |\t]*Content-Disposition[ |\t]*:)(.*)";
456	    private static final Pattern CONTENT_DISPOSITION_PATTERN = Pattern.compile(CONTENT_DISPOSITION_REGEX, Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE);
457	    private static final String CONTENT_TYPE_REGEX = "([ |\t]*content-type[ |\t]*:)(.*)";
458	    private static final Pattern CONTENT_TYPE_PATTERN = Pattern.compile(CONTENT_TYPE_REGEX, Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE);
459	    private static final String CONTENT_DISPOSITION_ATTRIBUTE_REGEX = "[ |\t]*([a-zA-Z]*)[ |\t]*=[ |\t]*['|\"]([^\"^']*)['|\"]";
460	    private static final Pattern CONTENT_DISPOSITION_ATTRIBUTE_PATTERN = Pattern.compile(CONTENT_DISPOSITION_ATTRIBUTE_REGEX);
461	    protected class HTTPSession implements IHTTPSession {
462	        private static final int REQUEST_BUFFER_LEN = 512;
463	        private static final int MEMORY_STORE_LIMIT = 1024;
464	        public static final int BUFSIZE = 8192;
465	        public static final int MAX_HEADER_SIZE = 1024;
466	        private final TempFileManager tempFileManager;
467	        private final OutputStream outputStream;
468	        private final BufferedInputStream inputStream;
469	        private int splitbyte;
470	        private int rlen;
471	        private String uri;
472	        private Method method;
473	        private Map<String, String> parms;
474	        private Map<String, String> headers;
475	        private CookieHandler cookies;
476	        private String queryParameterString;
477	        private String remoteIp;
478	        private String protocolVersion;
479	        public HTTPSession(TempFileManager tempFileManager, InputStream inputStream, OutputStream outputStream) {
480	            this.tempFileManager = tempFileManager;
481	            this.inputStream = new BufferedInputStream(inputStream, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
482	            this.outputStream = outputStream;
483	        }
484	        public HTTPSession(TempFileManager tempFileManager, InputStream inputStream, OutputStream outputStream, InetAddress inetAddress) {
485	            this.tempFileManager = tempFileManager;
486	            this.inputStream = new BufferedInputStream(inputStream, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
487	            this.outputStream = outputStream;
488	            this.remoteIp = inetAddress.isLoopbackAddress() || inetAddress.isAnyLocalAddress() ? "127.0.0.1" : inetAddress.getHostAddress().toString();
489	            this.headers = new HashMap<String, String>();
490	        }
491	        /**
492	         * Decodes the sent headers and loads the data into Key/value pairs
493	         */
494	        private void decodeHeader(BufferedReader in, Map<String, String> pre, Map<String, String> parms, Map<String, String> headers) throws ResponseException {
495	            try {
496	                // Read the request line
497	                String inLine = in.readLine();
498	                if (inLine == null) {
499	                    return;
500	                }
501	                StringTokenizer st = new StringTokenizer(inLine);
502	                if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {
503	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error. Usage: GET /example/file.html");
504	                }
505	                pre.put("method", st.nextToken());
506	                if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {
507	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Missing URI. Usage: GET /example/file.html");
508	                }
509	                String uri = st.nextToken();
510	                // Decode parameters from the URI
511	                int qmi = uri.indexOf('?');
512	                if (qmi >= 0) {
513	                    decodeParms(uri.substring(qmi + 1), parms);
514	                    uri = decodePercent(uri.substring(0, qmi));
515	                } else {
516	                    uri = decodePercent(uri);
517	                }
518	                // If there's another token, its protocol version,
519	                // followed by HTTP headers.
520	                // NOTE: this now forces header names lower case since they are
### CMD: awk NR>=521 && NR<=780 NanoHTTPD.java (numbered)
521	                // case insensitive and vary by client.
522	                if (st.hasMoreTokens()) {
523	                    protocolVersion = st.nextToken();
524	                } else {
525	                    protocolVersion = "HTTP/1.1";
526	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "no protocol version specified, strange. Assuming HTTP/1.1.");
527	                }
528	                String line = in.readLine();
529	                while (line != null && line.trim().length() > 0) {
530	                    int p = line.indexOf(':');
531	                    if (p >= 0) {
532	                        headers.put(line.substring(0, p).trim().toLowerCase(Locale.US), line.substring(p + 1).trim());
533	                    }
534	                    line = in.readLine();
535	                }
536	                pre.put("uri", uri);
537	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
538	                throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage(), ioe);
539	            }
540	        }
541	        /**
542	         * Decodes the Multipart Body data and put it into Key/Value pairs.
543	         */
544	        private void decodeMultipartFormData(String boundary, String encoding, ByteBuffer fbuf, Map<String, String> parms, Map<String, String> files) throws ResponseException {
545	            try {
546	                int[] boundary_idxs = getBoundaryPositions(fbuf, boundary.getBytes());
547	                if (boundary_idxs.length < 2) {
548	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Content type is multipart/form-data but contains less than two boundary strings.");
549	                }
550	                byte[] part_header_buff = new byte[MAX_HEADER_SIZE];
551	                for (int bi = 0; bi < boundary_idxs.length - 1; bi++) {
552	                    fbuf.position(boundary_idxs[bi]);
553	                    int len = (fbuf.remaining() < MAX_HEADER_SIZE) ? fbuf.remaining() : MAX_HEADER_SIZE;
554	                    fbuf.get(part_header_buff, 0, len);
555	                    BufferedReader in = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(new ByteArrayInputStream(part_header_buff, 0, len), Charset.forName(encoding)), len);
556	                    int headerLines = 0;
557	                    // First line is boundary string
558	                    String mpline = in.readLine();
559	                    headerLines++;
560	                    if (!mpline.contains(boundary)) {
561	                        throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Content type is multipart/form-data but chunk does not start with boundary.");
562	                    }
563	                    String part_name = null, file_name = null, content_type = null;
564	                    // Parse the reset of the header lines
565	                    mpline = in.readLine();
566	                    headerLines++;
567	                    while (mpline != null && mpline.trim().length() > 0) {
568	                        Matcher matcher = CONTENT_DISPOSITION_PATTERN.matcher(mpline);
569	                        if (matcher.matches()) {
570	                            String attributeString = matcher.group(2);
571	                            matcher = CONTENT_DISPOSITION_ATTRIBUTE_PATTERN.matcher(attributeString);
572	                            while (matcher.find()) {
573	                                String key = matcher.group(1);
574	                                if (key.equalsIgnoreCase("name")) {
575	                                    part_name = matcher.group(2);
576	                                } else if (key.equalsIgnoreCase("filename")) {
577	                                    file_name = matcher.group(2);
578	                                }
579	                            }
580	                        }
581	                        matcher = CONTENT_TYPE_PATTERN.matcher(mpline);
582	                        if (matcher.matches()) {
583	                            content_type = matcher.group(2).trim();
584	                        }
585	                        mpline = in.readLine();
586	                        headerLines++;
587	                    }
588	                    int part_header_len = 0;
589	                    while (headerLines-- > 0) {
590	                        part_header_len = scipOverNewLine(part_header_buff, part_header_len);
591	                    }
592	                    // Read the part data
593	                    if (part_header_len >= len - 4) {
594	                        throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, "Multipart header size exceeds MAX_HEADER_SIZE.");
595	                    }
596	                    int part_data_start = boundary_idxs[bi] + part_header_len;
597	                    int part_data_end = boundary_idxs[bi + 1] - 4;
598	                    fbuf.position(part_data_start);
599	                    if (content_type == null) {
600	                        // Read the part into a string
601	                        byte[] data_bytes = new byte[part_data_end - part_data_start];
602	                        fbuf.get(data_bytes);
603	                        parms.put(part_name, new String(data_bytes, encoding));
604	                    } else {
605	                        // Read it into a file
606	                        String path = saveTmpFile(fbuf, part_data_start, part_data_end - part_data_start, file_name);
607	                        if (!files.containsKey(part_name)) {
608	                            files.put(part_name, path);
609	                        } else {
610	                            int count = 2;
611	                            while (files.containsKey(part_name + count)) {
612	                                count++;
613	                            }
614	                            files.put(part_name + count, path);
615	                        }
616	                        parms.put(part_name, file_name);
617	                    }
618	                }
619	            } catch (ResponseException re) {
620	                throw re;
621	            } catch (Exception e) {
622	                throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, e.toString());
623	            }
624	        }
625	        private int scipOverNewLine(byte[] part_header_buff, int index) {
626	            while (part_header_buff[index] != '\n') {
627	                index++;
628	            }
629	            return ++index;
630	        }
631	        /**
632	         * Decodes parameters in percent-encoded URI-format ( e.g.
633	         * "name=Jack%20Daniels&pass=Single%20Malt" ) and adds them to given
634	         * Map. NOTE: this doesn't support multiple identical keys due to the
635	         * simplicity of Map.
636	         */
637	        private void decodeParms(String parms, Map<String, String> p) {
638	            if (parms == null) {
639	                this.queryParameterString = "";
640	                return;
641	            }
642	            this.queryParameterString = parms;
643	            StringTokenizer st = new StringTokenizer(parms, "&");
644	            while (st.hasMoreTokens()) {
645	                String e = st.nextToken();
646	                int sep = e.indexOf('=');
647	                if (sep >= 0) {
648	                    p.put(decodePercent(e.substring(0, sep)).trim(), decodePercent(e.substring(sep + 1)));
649	                } else {
650	                    p.put(decodePercent(e).trim(), "");
651	                }
652	            }
653	        }
654	        @Override
655	        public void execute() throws IOException {
656	            Response r = null;
657	            try {
658	                // Read the first 8192 bytes.
659	                // The full header should fit in here.
660	                // Apache's default header limit is 8KB.
661	                // Do NOT assume that a single read will get the entire header
662	                // at once!
663	                byte[] buf = new byte[HTTPSession.BUFSIZE];
664	                this.splitbyte = 0;
665	                this.rlen = 0;
666	                int read = -1;
667	                this.inputStream.mark(HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
668	                try {
669	                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, 0, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
670	                } catch (Exception e) {
671	                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
672	                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
673	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
674	                }
675	                if (read == -1) {
676	                    // socket was been closed
677	                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
678	                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
679	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
680	                }
681	                while (read > 0) {
682	                    this.rlen += read;
683	                    this.splitbyte = findHeaderEnd(buf, this.rlen);
684	                    if (this.splitbyte > 0) {
685	                        break;
686	                    }
687	                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, this.rlen, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE - this.rlen);
688	                }
689	                if (this.splitbyte < this.rlen) {
690	                    this.inputStream.reset();
691	                    this.inputStream.skip(this.splitbyte);
692	                }
693	                this.parms = new HashMap<String, String>();
694	                if (null == this.headers) {
695	                    this.headers = new HashMap<String, String>();
696	                } else {
697	                    this.headers.clear();
698	                }
699	                // Create a BufferedReader for parsing the header.
700	                BufferedReader hin = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(new ByteArrayInputStream(buf, 0, this.rlen)));
701	                // Decode the header into parms and header java properties
702	                Map<String, String> pre = new HashMap<String, String>();
703	                decodeHeader(hin, pre, this.parms, this.headers);
704	                if (null != this.remoteIp) {
705	                    this.headers.put("remote-addr", this.remoteIp);
706	                    this.headers.put("http-client-ip", this.remoteIp);
707	                }
708	                this.method = Method.lookup(pre.get("method"));
709	                if (this.method == null) {
710	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error.");
711	                }
712	                this.uri = pre.get("uri");
713	                this.cookies = new CookieHandler(this.headers);
714	                String connection = this.headers.get("connection");
715	                boolean keepAlive = protocolVersion.equals("HTTP/1.1") && (connection == null || !connection.matches("(?i).*close.*"));
716	                // Ok, now do the serve()
717	                // TODO: long body_size = getBodySize();
718	                // TODO: long pos_before_serve = this.inputStream.totalRead()
719	                // (requires implementaion for totalRead())
720	                r = serve(this);
721	                // TODO: this.inputStream.skip(body_size -
722	                // (this.inputStream.totalRead() - pos_before_serve))
723	                if (r == null) {
724	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: Serve() returned a null response.");
725	                } else {
726	                    String acceptEncoding = this.headers.get("accept-encoding");
727	                    this.cookies.unloadQueue(r);
728	                    r.setRequestMethod(this.method);
729	                    r.setGzipEncoding(useGzipWhenAccepted(r) && acceptEncoding != null && acceptEncoding.contains("gzip"));
730	                    r.setKeepAlive(keepAlive);
731	                    r.send(this.outputStream);
732	                }
733	                if (!keepAlive || "close".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getHeader("connection"))) {
734	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
735	                }
736	            } catch (SocketException e) {
737	                // throw it out to close socket object (finalAccept)
738	                throw e;
739	            } catch (SocketTimeoutException ste) {
740	                // treat socket timeouts the same way we treat socket exceptions
741	                // i.e. close the stream & finalAccept object by throwing the
742	                // exception up the call stack.
743	                throw ste;
744	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
745	                Response resp = newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage());
746	                resp.send(this.outputStream);
747	                safeClose(this.outputStream);
748	            } catch (ResponseException re) {
749	                Response resp = newFixedLengthResponse(re.getStatus(), NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, re.getMessage());
750	                resp.send(this.outputStream);
751	                safeClose(this.outputStream);
752	            } finally {
753	                safeClose(r);
754	                this.tempFileManager.clear();
755	            }
756	        }
757	        /**
758	         * Find byte index separating header from body. It must be the last byte
759	         * of the first two sequential new lines.
760	         */
761	        private int findHeaderEnd(final byte[] buf, int rlen) {
762	            int splitbyte = 0;
763	            while (splitbyte + 1 < rlen) {
764	                // RFC2616
765	                if (buf[splitbyte] == '\r' && buf[splitbyte + 1] == '\n' && splitbyte + 3 < rlen && buf[splitbyte + 2] == '\r' && buf[splitbyte + 3] == '\n') {
766	                    return splitbyte + 4;
767	                }
768	                // tolerance
769	                if (buf[splitbyte] == '\n' && buf[splitbyte + 1] == '\n') {
770	                    return splitbyte + 2;
771	                }
772	                splitbyte++;
773	            }
774	            return 0;
775	        }
776	        /**
777	         * Find the byte positions where multipart boundaries start. This reads
778	         * a large block at a time and uses a temporary buffer to optimize
779	         * (memory mapped) file access.
780	         */
### CMD: awk NR>=781 && NR<=1040 NanoHTTPD.java (numbered)
781	        private int[] getBoundaryPositions(ByteBuffer b, byte[] boundary) {
782	            int[] res = new int[0];
783	            if (b.remaining() < boundary.length) {
784	                return res;
785	            }
786	            int search_window_pos = 0;
787	            byte[] search_window = new byte[4 * 1024 + boundary.length];
788	            int first_fill = (b.remaining() < search_window.length) ? b.remaining() : search_window.length;
789	            b.get(search_window, 0, first_fill);
790	            int new_bytes = first_fill - boundary.length;
791	            do {
792	                // Search the search_window
793	                for (int j = 0; j < new_bytes; j++) {
794	                    for (int i = 0; i < boundary.length; i++) {
795	                        if (search_window[j + i] != boundary[i])
796	                            break;
797	                        if (i == boundary.length - 1) {
798	                            // Match found, add it to results
799	                            int[] new_res = new int[res.length + 1];
800	                            System.arraycopy(res, 0, new_res, 0, res.length);
801	                            new_res[res.length] = search_window_pos + j;
802	                            res = new_res;
803	                        }
804	                    }
805	                }
806	                search_window_pos += new_bytes;
807	                // Copy the end of the buffer to the start
808	                System.arraycopy(search_window, search_window.length - boundary.length, search_window, 0, boundary.length);
809	                // Refill search_window
810	                new_bytes = search_window.length - boundary.length;
811	                new_bytes = (b.remaining() < new_bytes) ? b.remaining() : new_bytes;
812	                b.get(search_window, boundary.length, new_bytes);
813	            } while (new_bytes > 0);
814	            return res;
815	        }
816	        @Override
817	        public CookieHandler getCookies() {
818	            return this.cookies;
819	        }
820	        @Override
821	        public final Map<String, String> getHeaders() {
822	            return this.headers;
823	        }
824	        @Override
825	        public final InputStream getInputStream() {
826	            return this.inputStream;
827	        }
828	        @Override
829	        public final Method getMethod() {
830	            return this.method;
831	        }
832	        @Override
833	        public final Map<String, String> getParms() {
834	            return this.parms;
835	        }
836	        @Override
837	        public String getQueryParameterString() {
838	            return this.queryParameterString;
839	        }
840	        private RandomAccessFile getTmpBucket() {
841	            try {
842	                TempFile tempFile = this.tempFileManager.createTempFile(null);
843	                return new RandomAccessFile(tempFile.getName(), "rw");
844	            } catch (Exception e) {
845	                throw new Error(e); // we won't recover, so throw an error
846	            }
847	        }
848	        @Override
849	        public final String getUri() {
850	            return this.uri;
851	        }
852	        /**
853	         * Deduce body length in bytes. Either from "content-length" header or
854	         * read bytes.
855	         */
856	        public long getBodySize() {
857	            if (this.headers.containsKey("content-length")) {
858	                return Long.parseLong(this.headers.get("content-length"));
859	            } else if (this.splitbyte < this.rlen) {
860	                return this.rlen - this.splitbyte;
861	            }
862	            return 0;
863	        }
864	        @Override
865	        public void parseBody(Map<String, String> files) throws IOException, ResponseException {
866	            RandomAccessFile randomAccessFile = null;
867	            try {
868	                long size = getBodySize();
869	                ByteArrayOutputStream baos = null;
870	                DataOutput request_data_output = null;
871	                // Store the request in memory or a file, depending on size
872	                if (size < MEMORY_STORE_LIMIT) {
873	                    baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
874	                    request_data_output = new DataOutputStream(baos);
875	                } else {
876	                    randomAccessFile = getTmpBucket();
877	                    request_data_output = randomAccessFile;
878	                }
879	                // Read all the body and write it to request_data_output
880	                byte[] buf = new byte[REQUEST_BUFFER_LEN];
881	                while (this.rlen >= 0 && size > 0) {
882	                    this.rlen = this.inputStream.read(buf, 0, (int) Math.min(size, REQUEST_BUFFER_LEN));
883	                    size -= this.rlen;
884	                    if (this.rlen > 0) {
885	                        request_data_output.write(buf, 0, this.rlen);
886	                    }
887	                }
888	                ByteBuffer fbuf = null;
889	                if (baos != null) {
890	                    fbuf = ByteBuffer.wrap(baos.toByteArray(), 0, baos.size());
891	                } else {
892	                    fbuf = randomAccessFile.getChannel().map(FileChannel.MapMode.READ_ONLY, 0, randomAccessFile.length());
893	                    randomAccessFile.seek(0);
894	                }
895	                // If the method is POST, there may be parameters
896	                // in data section, too, read it:
897	                if (Method.POST.equals(this.method)) {
898	                    String contentType = "";
899	                    String contentTypeHeader = this.headers.get("content-type");
900	                    StringTokenizer st = null;
901	                    if (contentTypeHeader != null) {
902	                        st = new StringTokenizer(contentTypeHeader, ",; ");
903	                        if (st.hasMoreTokens()) {
904	                            contentType = st.nextToken();
905	                        }
906	                    }
907	                    if ("multipart/form-data".equalsIgnoreCase(contentType)) {
908	                        // Handle multipart/form-data
909	                        if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {
910	                            throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST,
911	                                    "BAD REQUEST: Content type is multipart/form-data but boundary missing. Usage: GET /example/file.html");
912	                        }
913	                        decodeMultipartFormData(getAttributeFromContentHeader(contentTypeHeader, BOUNDARY_PATTERN, null), //
914	                                getAttributeFromContentHeader(contentTypeHeader, CHARSET_PATTERN, "US-ASCII"), fbuf, this.parms, files);
915	                    } else {
916	                        byte[] postBytes = new byte[fbuf.remaining()];
917	                        fbuf.get(postBytes);
918	                        String postLine = new String(postBytes).trim();
919	                        // Handle application/x-www-form-urlencoded
920	                        if ("application/x-www-form-urlencoded".equalsIgnoreCase(contentType)) {
921	                            decodeParms(postLine, this.parms);
922	                        } else if (postLine.length() != 0) {
923	                            // Special case for raw POST data => create a
924	                            // special files entry "postData" with raw content
925	                            // data
926	                            files.put("postData", postLine);
927	                        }
928	                    }
929	                } else if (Method.PUT.equals(this.method)) {
930	                    files.put("content", saveTmpFile(fbuf, 0, fbuf.limit(), null));
931	                }
932	            } finally {
933	                safeClose(randomAccessFile);
934	            }
935	        }
936	        private String getAttributeFromContentHeader(String contentTypeHeader, Pattern pattern, String defaultValue) {
937	            Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher(contentTypeHeader);
938	            return matcher.find() ? matcher.group(2) : defaultValue;
939	        }
940	        /**
941	         * Retrieves the content of a sent file and saves it to a temporary
942	         * file. The full path to the saved file is returned.
943	         */
944	        private String saveTmpFile(ByteBuffer b, int offset, int len, String filename_hint) {
945	            String path = "";
946	            if (len > 0) {
947	                FileOutputStream fileOutputStream = null;
948	                try {
949	                    TempFile tempFile = this.tempFileManager.createTempFile(filename_hint);
950	                    ByteBuffer src = b.duplicate();
951	                    fileOutputStream = new FileOutputStream(tempFile.getName());
952	                    FileChannel dest = fileOutputStream.getChannel();
953	                    src.position(offset).limit(offset + len);
954	                    dest.write(src.slice());
955	                    path = tempFile.getName();
956	                } catch (Exception e) { // Catch exception if any
957	                    throw new Error(e); // we won't recover, so throw an error
958	                } finally {
959	                    safeClose(fileOutputStream);
960	                }
961	            }
962	            return path;
963	        }
964	    }

989	    /**
990	     * HTTP Request methods, with the ability to decode a <code>String</code>
991	     * back to its enum value.
992	     */
993	    public enum Method {
994	        GET,
995	        PUT,
996	        POST,
997	        DELETE,
998	        HEAD,
999	        OPTIONS,
1000	        TRACE,
1001	        CONNECT,
1002	        PATCH;
1003	        static Method lookup(String method) {
1004	            for (Method m : Method.values()) {
1005	                if (m.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(method)) {
1006	                    return m;
1007	                }
1008	            }
1009	            return null;
1010	        }

1012	    /**
1013	     * HTTP response. Return one of these from serve().
1014	     */
1015	    public static class Response implements Closeable {
1016	        public interface IStatus {
1017	            String getDescription();
1018	            int getRequestStatus();
1019	        }
1020	        /**
1021	         * Some HTTP response status codes
1022	         */
1023	        public enum Status implements IStatus {
1024	            SWITCH_PROTOCOL(101, "Switching Protocols"),
1025	            OK(200, "OK"),
1026	            CREATED(201, "Created"),
1027	            ACCEPTED(202, "Accepted"),
1028	            NO_CONTENT(204, "No Content"),
1029	            PARTIAL_CONTENT(206, "Partial Content"),
1030	            REDIRECT(301, "Moved Permanently"),
1031	            TEMPORARY_REDIRECT(302, "Moved Temporarily"),
1032	            NOT_MODIFIED(304, "Not Modified"),
1033	            BAD_REQUEST(400, "Bad Request"),
1034	            UNAUTHORIZED(401, "Unauthorized"),
1035	            FORBIDDEN(403, "Forbidden"),
1036	            NOT_FOUND(404, "Not Found"),
1037	            METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED(405, "Method Not Allowed"),
1038	            NOT_ACCEPTABLE(406, "Not Acceptable"),
1039	            REQUEST_TIMEOUT(408, "Request Timeout"),
1040	            CONFLICT(409, "Conflict"),
### CMD: awk NR>=1041 && NR<=1300 NanoHTTPD.java (numbered)
1041	            RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE(416, "Requested Range Not Satisfiable"),
1042	            INTERNAL_ERROR(500, "Internal Server Error"),
1043	            NOT_IMPLEMENTED(501, "Not Implemented"),
1044	            UNSUPPORTED_HTTP_VERSION(505, "HTTP Version Not Supported");
1045	            private final int requestStatus;
1046	            private final String description;
1047	            Status(int requestStatus, String description) {
1048	                this.requestStatus = requestStatus;
1049	                this.description = description;
1050	            }
1051	            @Override
1052	            public String getDescription() {
1053	                return "" + this.requestStatus + " " + this.description;
1054	            }
1055	            @Override
1056	            public int getRequestStatus() {
1057	                return this.requestStatus;
1058	            }
1059	        }
1060	        /**
1061	         * Output stream that will automatically send every write to the wrapped
1062	         * OutputStream according to chunked transfer:
1063	         * http://www.w3.org/Protocols/rfc2616/rfc2616-sec3.html#sec3.6.1
1064	         */
1065	        private static class ChunkedOutputStream extends FilterOutputStream {
1066	            public ChunkedOutputStream(OutputStream out) {
1067	                super(out);
1068	            }
1069	            @Override
1070	            public void write(int b) throws IOException {
1071	                byte[] data = {
1072	                        (byte) b
1073	                };
1074	                write(data, 0, 1);
1075	            }
1076	            @Override
1077	            public void write(byte[] b) throws IOException {
1078	                write(b, 0, b.length);
1079	            }
1080	            @Override
1081	            public void write(byte[] b, int off, int len) throws IOException {
1082	                if (len == 0)
1083	                    return;
1084	                out.write(String.format("%x\r\n", len).getBytes());
1085	                out.write(b, off, len);
1086	                out.write("\r\n".getBytes());
1087	            }
1088	            public void finish() throws IOException {
1089	                out.write("0\r\n\r\n".getBytes());
1090	            }
1091	        }
1092	        /**
1093	         * HTTP status code after processing, e.g. "200 OK", Status.OK
1094	         */
1095	        private IStatus status;
1096	        /**
1097	         * MIME type of content, e.g. "text/html"
1098	         */
1099	        private String mimeType;
1100	        /**
1101	         * Data of the response, may be null.
1102	         */
1103	        private InputStream data;
1104	        private long contentLength;
1105	        /**
1106	         * Headers for the HTTP response. Use addHeader() to add lines.
1107	         */
1108	        private final Map<String, String> header = new HashMap<String, String>();
1109	        /**
1110	         * The request method that spawned this response.
1111	         */
1112	        private Method requestMethod;
1113	        /**
1114	         * Use chunkedTransfer
1115	         */
1116	        private boolean chunkedTransfer;
1117	        private boolean encodeAsGzip;
1118	        private boolean keepAlive;
1119	        /**
1120	         * Creates a fixed length response if totalBytes>=0, otherwise chunked.
1121	         */
1122	        protected Response(IStatus status, String mimeType, InputStream data, long totalBytes) {
1123	            this.status = status;
1124	            this.mimeType = mimeType;
1125	            if (data == null) {
1126	                this.data = new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]);
1127	                this.contentLength = 0L;
1128	            } else {
1129	                this.data = data;
1130	                this.contentLength = totalBytes;
1131	            }
1132	            this.chunkedTransfer = this.contentLength < 0;
1133	            keepAlive = true;
1134	        }
1135	        @Override
1136	        public void close() throws IOException {
1137	            if (this.data != null) {
1138	                this.data.close();
1139	            }
1140	        }
1141	        /**
1142	         * Adds given line to the header.
1143	         */
1144	        public void addHeader(String name, String value) {
1145	            this.header.put(name, value);
1146	        }
1147	        public InputStream getData() {
1148	            return this.data;
1149	        }
1150	        public String getHeader(String name) {
1151	            for (String headerName : header.keySet()) {
1152	                if (headerName.equalsIgnoreCase(name)) {
1153	                    return header.get(headerName);
1154	                }
1155	            }
1156	            return null;
1157	        }
1158	        public String getMimeType() {
1159	            return this.mimeType;
1160	        }
1161	        public Method getRequestMethod() {
1162	            return this.requestMethod;
1163	        }
1164	        public IStatus getStatus() {
1165	            return this.status;
1166	        }
1167	        public void setGzipEncoding(boolean encodeAsGzip) {
1168	            this.encodeAsGzip = encodeAsGzip;
1169	        }
1170	        public void setKeepAlive(boolean useKeepAlive) {
1171	            this.keepAlive = useKeepAlive;
1172	        }
1173	        private static boolean headerAlreadySent(Map<String, String> header, String name) {
1174	            boolean alreadySent = false;
1175	            for (String headerName : header.keySet()) {
1176	                alreadySent |= headerName.equalsIgnoreCase(name);
1177	            }
1178	            return alreadySent;
1179	        }
1180	        /**
1181	         * Sends given response to the socket.
1182	         */
1183	        protected void send(OutputStream outputStream) {
1184	            String mime = this.mimeType;
1185	            SimpleDateFormat gmtFrmt = new SimpleDateFormat("E, d MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss 'GMT'", Locale.US);
1186	            gmtFrmt.setTimeZone(TimeZone.getTimeZone("GMT"));
1187	            try {
1188	                if (this.status == null) {
1189	                    throw new Error("sendResponse(): Status can't be null.");
1190	                }
1191	                PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(outputStream, "UTF-8")), false);
1192	                pw.print("HTTP/1.1 " + this.status.getDescription() + " \r\n");
1193	                if (mime != null) {
1194	                    pw.print("Content-Type: " + mime + "\r\n");
1195	                }
1196	                if (this.header == null || this.header.get("Date") == null) {
1197	                    pw.print("Date: " + gmtFrmt.format(new Date()) + "\r\n");
1198	                }
1199	                if (this.header != null) {
1200	                    for (String key : this.header.keySet()) {
1201	                        String value = this.header.get(key);
1202	                        pw.print(key + ": " + value + "\r\n");
1203	                    }
1204	                }
1205	                if (!headerAlreadySent(header, "connection")) {
1206	                    pw.print("Connection: " + (this.keepAlive ? "keep-alive" : "close") + "\r\n");
1207	                }
1208	                if (headerAlreadySent(this.header, "content-length")) {
1209	                    encodeAsGzip = false;
1210	                }
1211	                if (encodeAsGzip) {
1212	                    pw.print("Content-Encoding: gzip\r\n");
1213	                    setChunkedTransfer(true);
1214	                }
1215	                long pending = this.data != null ? this.contentLength : 0;
1216	                if (this.requestMethod != Method.HEAD && this.chunkedTransfer) {
1217	                    pw.print("Transfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n");
1218	                } else if (!encodeAsGzip) {
1219	                    pending = sendContentLengthHeaderIfNotAlreadyPresent(pw, this.header, pending);
1220	                }
1221	                pw.print("\r\n");
1222	                pw.flush();
1223	                sendBodyWithCorrectTransferAndEncoding(outputStream, pending);
1224	                outputStream.flush();
1225	                safeClose(this.data);
1226	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
1227	                NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not send response to the client", ioe);
1228	            }
1229	        }
1230	        private void sendBodyWithCorrectTransferAndEncoding(OutputStream outputStream, long pending) throws IOException {
1231	            if (this.requestMethod != Method.HEAD && this.chunkedTransfer) {
1232	                ChunkedOutputStream chunkedOutputStream = new ChunkedOutputStream(outputStream);
1233	                sendBodyWithCorrectEncoding(chunkedOutputStream, -1);
1234	                chunkedOutputStream.finish();
1235	            } else {
1236	                sendBodyWithCorrectEncoding(outputStream, pending);
1237	            }
1238	        }
1239	        private void sendBodyWithCorrectEncoding(OutputStream outputStream, long pending) throws IOException {
1240	            if (encodeAsGzip) {
1241	                GZIPOutputStream gzipOutputStream = new GZIPOutputStream(outputStream);
1242	                sendBody(gzipOutputStream, -1);
1243	                gzipOutputStream.finish();
1244	            } else {
1245	                sendBody(outputStream, pending);
1246	            }
1247	        }
1248	        /**
1249	         * Sends the body to the specified OutputStream. The pending parameter
1250	         * limits the maximum amounts of bytes sent unless it is -1, in which
1251	         * case everything is sent.
1252	         *
1253	         * @param outputStream
1254	         *            the OutputStream to send data to
1255	         * @param pending
1256	         *            -1 to send everything, otherwise sets a max limit to the
1257	         *            number of bytes sent
1258	         * @throws IOException
1259	         *             if something goes wrong while sending the data.
1260	         */
1261	        private void sendBody(OutputStream outputStream, long pending) throws IOException {
1262	            long BUFFER_SIZE = 16 * 1024;
1263	            byte[] buff = new byte[(int) BUFFER_SIZE];
1264	            boolean sendEverything = pending == -1;
1265	            while (pending > 0 || sendEverything) {
1266	                long bytesToRead = sendEverything ? BUFFER_SIZE : Math.min(pending, BUFFER_SIZE);
1267	                int read = this.data.read(buff, 0, (int) bytesToRead);
1268	                if (read <= 0) {
1269	                    break;
1270	                }
1271	                outputStream.write(buff, 0, read);
1272	                if (!sendEverything) {
1273	                    pending -= read;
1274	                }
1275	            }
1276	        }
1277	        protected static long sendContentLengthHeaderIfNotAlreadyPresent(PrintWriter pw, Map<String, String> header, long size) {
1278	            for (String headerName : header.keySet()) {
1279	                if (headerName.equalsIgnoreCase("content-length")) {
1280	                    try {
1281	                        return Long.parseLong(header.get(headerName));
1282	                    } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
1283	                        return size;
1284	                    }
1285	                }
1286	            }
1287	            pw.print("Content-Length: " + size + "\r\n");
1288	            return size;
1289	        }
1290	        public void setChunkedTransfer(boolean chunkedTransfer) {
1291	            this.chunkedTransfer = chunkedTransfer;
1292	        }
1293	        public void setData(InputStream data) {
1294	            this.data = data;
1295	        }
1296	        public void setMimeType(String mimeType) {
1297	            this.mimeType = mimeType;
1298	        }
1299	        public void setRequestMethod(Method requestMethod) {
1300	            this.requestMethod = requestMethod;
### CMD: awk NR>=1301 && NR<=1560 NanoHTTPD.java (numbered)
1301	        }
1302	        public void setStatus(IStatus status) {
1303	            this.status = status;
1304	        }
1305	    }
1306	    public static final class ResponseException extends Exception {
1307	        private static final long serialVersionUID = 6569838532917408380L;
1308	        private final Response.Status status;
1309	        public ResponseException(Response.Status status, String message) {
1310	            super(message);
1311	            this.status = status;
1312	        }
1313	        public ResponseException(Response.Status status, String message, Exception e) {
1314	            super(message, e);
1315	            this.status = status;
1316	        }

1321	    /**
1322	     * The runnable that will be used for the main listening thread.
1323	     */
1324	    public class ServerRunnable implements Runnable {
1325	        private final int timeout;
1326	        private IOException bindException;
1327	        private boolean hasBinded = false;
1328	        private ServerRunnable(int timeout) {
1329	            this.timeout = timeout;
1330	        }
1331	        @Override
1332	        public void run() {
1333	            try {
1334	                myServerSocket.bind(hostname != null ? new InetSocketAddress(hostname, myPort) : new InetSocketAddress(myPort));
1335	                hasBinded = true;
1336	            } catch (IOException e) {
1337	                this.bindException = e;
1338	                return;
1339	            }
1340	            do {
1341	                try {
1342	                    final Socket finalAccept = NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.accept();
1343	                    if (this.timeout > 0) {
1344	                        finalAccept.setSoTimeout(this.timeout);
1345	                    }
1346	                    final InputStream inputStream = finalAccept.getInputStream();
1347	                    NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.exec(createClientHandler(finalAccept, inputStream));
1348	                } catch (IOException e) {
1349	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
1350	                }
1351	            } while (!NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.isClosed());
1352	        }
1353	    }

1392	     * Maximum time to wait on Socket.getInputStream().read() (in milliseconds)
1393	     * This is required as the Keep-Alive HTTP connections would otherwise block

1396	    public static final int SOCKET_READ_TIMEOUT = 5000;

1399	     */
1400	    public static final String MIME_PLAINTEXT = "text/plain";

1405	    /**
1406	     * Pseudo-Parameter to use to store the actual query string in the

1409	    private static final String QUERY_STRING_PARAMETER = "NanoHttpd.QUERY_STRING";

1411	     * logger to log to.
1412	     */
1413	    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(NanoHTTPD.class.getName());
1414	    /**
1415	     * Hashtable mapping (String)FILENAME_EXTENSION -> (String)MIME_TYPE
1416	     */
1417	    protected static Map<String, String> MIME_TYPES;
1418	    public static Map<String, String> mimeTypes() {
1419	        if (MIME_TYPES == null) {
1420	            MIME_TYPES = new HashMap<String, String>();
1421	            loadMimeTypes(MIME_TYPES, "META-INF/nanohttpd/default-mimetypes.properties");
1422	            loadMimeTypes(MIME_TYPES, "META-INF/nanohttpd/mimetypes.properties");
1423	            if (MIME_TYPES.isEmpty()) {
1424	                LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "no mime types found in the classpath! please provide mimetypes.properties");
1425	            }
1426	        }
1427	        return MIME_TYPES;
1428	    }
1429	    private static void loadMimeTypes(Map<String, String> result, String resourceName) {
1430	        try {
1431	            Enumeration<URL> resources = NanoHTTPD.class.getClassLoader().getResources(resourceName);
1432	            while (resources.hasMoreElements()) {
1433	                URL url = (URL) resources.nextElement();
1434	                Properties properties = new Properties();
1435	                InputStream stream = null;
1436	                try {
1437	                    stream = url.openStream();
1438	                    properties.load(url.openStream());
1439	                } catch (IOException e) {
1440	                    LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "could not load mimetypes from " + url, e);
1441	                } finally {
1442	                    safeClose(stream);
1443	                }
1444	                result.putAll((Map) properties);
1445	            }
1446	        } catch (IOException e) {
1447	            LOG.log(Level.INFO, "no mime types available at " + resourceName);
1448	        }
1449	    };
1450	    /**
1451	     * Creates an SSLSocketFactory for HTTPS. Pass a loaded KeyStore and an
1452	     * array of loaded KeyManagers. These objects must properly
1453	     * loaded/initialized by the caller.
1454	     */
1455	    public static SSLServerSocketFactory makeSSLSocketFactory(KeyStore loadedKeyStore, KeyManager[] keyManagers) throws IOException {
1456	        SSLServerSocketFactory res = null;
1457	        try {
1458	            TrustManagerFactory trustManagerFactory = TrustManagerFactory.getInstance(TrustManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm());
1459	            trustManagerFactory.init(loadedKeyStore);
1460	            SSLContext ctx = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS");
1461	            ctx.init(keyManagers, trustManagerFactory.getTrustManagers(), null);
1462	            res = ctx.getServerSocketFactory();
1463	        } catch (Exception e) {
1464	            throw new IOException(e.getMessage());
1465	        }
1466	        return res;
1467	    }
1468	    /**
1469	     * Creates an SSLSocketFactory for HTTPS. Pass a loaded KeyStore and a
1470	     * loaded KeyManagerFactory. These objects must properly loaded/initialized
1471	     * by the caller.
1472	     */
1473	    public static SSLServerSocketFactory makeSSLSocketFactory(KeyStore loadedKeyStore, KeyManagerFactory loadedKeyFactory) throws IOException {
1474	        try {
1475	            return makeSSLSocketFactory(loadedKeyStore, loadedKeyFactory.getKeyManagers());
1476	        } catch (Exception e) {
1477	            throw new IOException(e.getMessage());
1478	        }
1479	    }
1480	    /**
1481	     * Creates an SSLSocketFactory for HTTPS. Pass a KeyStore resource with your
1482	     * certificate and passphrase
1483	     */
1484	    public static SSLServerSocketFactory makeSSLSocketFactory(String keyAndTrustStoreClasspathPath, char[] passphrase) throws IOException {
1485	        try {
1486	            KeyStore keystore = KeyStore.getInstance(KeyStore.getDefaultType());
1487	            InputStream keystoreStream = NanoHTTPD.class.getResourceAsStream(keyAndTrustStoreClasspathPath);
1488	            keystore.load(keystoreStream, passphrase);
1489	            KeyManagerFactory keyManagerFactory = KeyManagerFactory.getInstance(KeyManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm());
1490	            keyManagerFactory.init(keystore, passphrase);
1491	            return makeSSLSocketFactory(keystore, keyManagerFactory);
1492	        } catch (Exception e) {
1493	            throw new IOException(e.getMessage());
1494	        }
1495	    }
1496	    /**
1497	     * Get MIME type from file name extension, if possible
1498	     *
1499	     * @param uri
1500	     *            the string representing a file
1501	     * @return the connected mime/type
1502	     */
1503	    public static String getMimeTypeForFile(String uri) {
1504	        int dot = uri.lastIndexOf('.');
1505	        String mime = null;
1506	        if (dot >= 0) {
1507	            mime = mimeTypes().get(uri.substring(dot + 1).toLowerCase());
1508	        }
1509	        return mime == null ? "application/octet-stream" : mime;
1510	    }
1511	    private static final void safeClose(Object closeable) {
1512	        try {
1513	            if (closeable != null) {
1514	                if (closeable instanceof Closeable) {
1515	                    ((Closeable) closeable).close();
1516	                } else if (closeable instanceof Socket) {
1517	                    ((Socket) closeable).close();
1518	                } else if (closeable instanceof ServerSocket) {
1519	                    ((ServerSocket) closeable).close();
1520	                } else {
1521	                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown object to close");
1522	                }
1523	            }
1524	        } catch (IOException e) {
1525	            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not close", e);
1526	        }
1527	    }

1593	    /**
1594	     * Decode parameters from a URL, handing the case where a single parameter
1595	     * name might have been supplied several times, by return lists of values.
1596	     * In general these lists will contain a single element.
1597	     *
1598	     * @param parms
1599	     *            original <b>NanoHTTPD</b> parameters values, as passed to the
1600	     *            <code>serve()</code> method.
1601	     * @return a map of <code>String</code> (parameter name) to
1602	     *         <code>List&lt;String&gt;</code> (a list of the values supplied).
1603	     */
1604	    protected static Map<String, List<String>> decodeParameters(Map<String, String> parms) {
1605	        return decodeParameters(parms.get(NanoHTTPD.QUERY_STRING_PARAMETER));
1606	    }
1607	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1608	    // //
1609	    /**
1610	     * Decode parameters from a URL, handing the case where a single parameter
1611	     * name might have been supplied several times, by return lists of values.
1612	     * In general these lists will contain a single element.
1613	     *
1614	     * @param queryString
1615	     *            a query string pulled from the URL.
1616	     * @return a map of <code>String</code> (parameter name) to
1617	     *         <code>List&lt;String&gt;</code> (a list of the values supplied).
1618	     */
1619	    protected static Map<String, List<String>> decodeParameters(String queryString) {
1620	        Map<String, List<String>> parms = new HashMap<String, List<String>>();
1621	        if (queryString != null) {
1622	            StringTokenizer st = new StringTokenizer(queryString, "&");
1623	            while (st.hasMoreTokens()) {
1624	                String e = st.nextToken();
1625	                int sep = e.indexOf('=');
1626	                String propertyName = sep >= 0 ? decodePercent(e.substring(0, sep)).trim() : decodePercent(e).trim();
1627	                if (!parms.containsKey(propertyName)) {
1628	                    parms.put(propertyName, new ArrayList<String>());
1629	                }
1630	                String propertyValue = sep >= 0 ? decodePercent(e.substring(sep + 1)) : null;
1631	                if (propertyValue != null) {
1632	                    parms.get(propertyName).add(propertyValue);
1633	                }
1634	            }
1635	        }
1636	        return parms;
1637	    }
1638	    /**
1639	     * Decode percent encoded <code>String</code> values.
1640	     *
1641	     * @param str
1642	     *            the percent encoded <code>String</code>
1643	     * @return expanded form of the input, for example "foo%20bar" becomes
1644	     *         "foo bar"
1645	     */
1646	    protected static String decodePercent(String str) {
1647	        String decoded = null;
1648	        try {
1649	            decoded = URLDecoder.decode(str, "UTF8");
1650	        } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException ignored) {
1651	            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "Encoding not supported, ignored", ignored);
1652	        }
1653	        return decoded;
1654	    }
1655	    /**
1656	     * @return true if the gzip compression should be used if the client
1657	     *         accespts it. Default this option is on for text content and off
1658	     *         for everything. Override this for custom semantics.
1659	     */
1660	    protected boolean useGzipWhenAccepted(Response r) {
1661	        return r.getMimeType() != null && r.getMimeType().toLowerCase().contains("text/");
1662	    }
