1699	    /**
1700	     * Create a text response with known length.
1701	     */
1702	    public static Response newFixedLengthResponse(Response.IStatus status, String mimeType, String txt) {
1703	        if (txt == null) {
1704	            return newFixedLengthResponse(status, mimeType, new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]), 0);
1705	        } else {
1706	            byte[] bytes;
1707	            try {
1708	                bytes = txt.getBytes("UTF-8");
1709	            } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException e) {
1710	                NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "encoding problem, responding nothing", e);
1711	                bytes = new byte[0];
1712	            }
1713	            return newFixedLengthResponse(status, mimeType, new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes), bytes.length);
1714	        }
1715	    }
1716	    /**
1717	     * Create a text response with known length.
1718	     */
1719	    public static Response newFixedLengthResponse(String msg) {
1720	        return newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.OK, NanoHTTPD.MIME_HTML, msg);
1721	    }
1722	    /**
1723	     * Override this to customize the server.
1724	     * <p/>
1725	     * <p/>
1726	     * (By default, this returns a 404 "Not Found" plain text error response.)
1727	     *
1728	     * @param session
1729	     *            The HTTP session
1730	     * @return HTTP response, see class Response for details
1731	     */
1732	    public Response serve(IHTTPSession session) {
1733	        Map<String, String> files = new HashMap<String, String>();
1734	        Method method = session.getMethod();
1735	        if (Method.PUT.equals(method) || Method.POST.equals(method)) {
1736	            try {
1737	                session.parseBody(files);
1738	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
1739	                return newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage());
1740	            } catch (ResponseException re) {
1741	                return newFixedLengthResponse(re.getStatus(), NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, re.getMessage());
1742	            }
1743	        }
1744	        Map<String, String> parms = session.getParms();
1745	        parms.put(NanoHTTPD.QUERY_STRING_PARAMETER, session.getQueryParameterString());
1746	        return serve(session.getUri(), method, session.getHeaders(), parms, files);
1747	    }
1748	    /**
1749	     * Override this to customize the server.
1750	     * <p/>

1752	     * (By default, this returns a 404 "Not Found" plain text error response.)
1753	     *
1754	     * @param uri

1761	     *            POST, data.

1766	    @Deprecated

1799	     */
1800	    public void start(final int timeout) throws IOException {
1801	        start(timeout, true);
1802	    }
1803	    /**
1804	     * Start the server.
1805	     *
1806	     * @param timeout
1807	     *            timeout to use for socket connections.
1808	     * @param daemon
1809	     *            start the thread daemon or not.
1810	     * @throws IOException
1811	     *             if the socket is in use.
1812	     */
1813	    public void start(final int timeout, boolean daemon) throws IOException {
1814	        this.myServerSocket = this.getServerSocketFactory().create();
1815	        this.myServerSocket.setReuseAddress(true);
1816	        ServerRunnable serverRunnable = createServerRunnable(timeout);
1817	        this.myThread = new Thread(serverRunnable);
1818	        this.myThread.setDaemon(daemon);
1819	        this.myThread.setName("NanoHttpd Main Listener");
1820	        this.myThread.start();
1821	        while (!serverRunnable.hasBinded && serverRunnable.bindException == null) {
1822	            try {
1823	                Thread.sleep(10L);
1824	            } catch (Throwable e) {
1825	                // on android this may not be allowed, that's why we
1826	                // catch throwable the wait should be very short because we are
1827	                // just waiting for the bind of the socket
1828	            }
1829	        }
1830	        if (serverRunnable.bindException != null) {
1831	            throw serverRunnable.bindException;
1832	        }
1833	    }
1834	    /**
1835	     * Stop the server.
1836	     */
1837	    public void stop() {
1838	        try {
1839	            safeClose(this.myServerSocket);
1840	            this.asyncRunner.closeAll();
1841	            if (this.myThread != null) {
1842	                this.myThread.join();
1843	            }
1844	        } catch (Exception e) {
1845	            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not stop all connections", e);
1846	        }
1847	    }
1848	    public final boolean wasStarted() {
1849	        return this.myServerSocket != null && this.myThread != null;
1850	    }

### CMD: sed -n 226,300p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 226 (CookieHandler)
   226	     * features.
   227	     *
   228	     * @author LordFokas
   229	     */
   230	    public class CookieHandler implements Iterable<String> {
   231	        private final HashMap<String, String> cookies = new HashMap<String, String>();
   232	        private final ArrayList<Cookie> queue = new ArrayList<Cookie>();
   233	        public CookieHandler(Map<String, String> httpHeaders) {
   234	            String raw = httpHeaders.get("cookie");
   235	            if (raw != null) {
   236	                String[] tokens = raw.split(";");
   237	                for (String token : tokens) {
   238	                    String[] data = token.trim().split("=");
   239	                    if (data.length == 2) {
   240	                        this.cookies.put(data[0], data[1]);
   241	                    }
   242	                }
   243	            }
   244	        }
   245	        /**
   246	         * Set a cookie with an expiration date from a month ago, effectively
   247	         * deleting it on the client side.
   248	         *
   249	         * @param name
   250	         *            The cookie name.
   251	         */
   252	        public void delete(String name) {
   253	            set(name, "-delete-", -30);
   254	        }
   255	        @NonNull
   256	        @Override
   257	        public Iterator<String> iterator() {
   258	            return this.cookies.keySet().iterator();
   259	        }
   260	        /**
   261	         * Read a cookie from the HTTP Headers.
   262	         *
   263	         * @param name
   264	         *            The cookie's name.
   265	         * @return The cookie's value if it exists, null otherwise.
   266	         */
   267	        public String read(String name) {
   268	            return this.cookies.get(name);
   269	        }
   270	        public void set(Cookie cookie) {
   271	            this.queue.add(cookie);
   272	        }
   273	        /**
   274	         * Sets a cookie.
   275	         *
   276	         * @param name
   277	         *            The cookie's name.
   278	         * @param value
   279	         *            The cookie's value.
   280	         * @param expires
   281	         *            How many days until the cookie expires.
   282	         */
   283	        public void set(String name, String value, int expires) {
   284	            this.queue.add(new Cookie(name, value, Cookie.getHTTPTime(expires)));
   285	        }
   286	        /**
   287	         * Internally used by the webserver to add all queued cookies into the
   288	         * Response's HTTP Headers.
   289	         *
   290	         * @param response
   291	         *            The Response object to which headers the queued cookies
   292	         *            will be added.
   293	         */
   294	        public void unloadQueue(Response response) {
   295	            for (Cookie cookie : this.queue) {
   296	                response.addHeader("Set-Cookie", cookie.getHTTPHeader());
   297	            }
   298	        }
   299	    }

### CMD: sed -n 305,340p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 305 (DefaultAsyncRunner)
   305	     * These are set to <i>daemon</i> status, and named according to the request
   306	     * number. The name is useful when profiling the application.
   307	     * </p>
   308	     */
   309	    public static class DefaultAsyncRunner implements AsyncRunner {
   310	        private long requestCount;
   311	        private final List<ClientHandler> running = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<NanoHTTPD.ClientHandler>());
   312	        /**
   313	         * @return a list with currently running clients.
   314	         */
   315	        public List<ClientHandler> getRunning() {
   316	            return running;
   317	        }
   318	        @Override
   319	        public void closeAll() {
   320	            // copy of the list for concurrency
   321	            for (ClientHandler clientHandler : new ArrayList<ClientHandler>(this.running)) {
   322	                clientHandler.close();
   323	            }
   324	        }
   325	        @Override
   326	        public void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
   327	            this.running.remove(clientHandler);
   328	        }
   329	        @Override
   330	        public void exec(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
   331	            ++this.requestCount;
   332	            Thread t = new Thread(clientHandler);
   333	            t.setDaemon(true);
   334	            t.setName("NanoHttpd Request Processor (#" + this.requestCount + ")");
   335	            this.running.add(clientHandler);
   336	            t.start();
   337	        }
   338	    }

   392	            for (TempFile file : this.tempFiles) {
   393	                try {
   394	                    file.delete();
   395	                } catch (Exception ignored) {
   396	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "could not delete file ", ignored);
   397	                }
   398	            }
   399	            this.tempFiles.clear();
   400	        }
   401	        @Override
   402	        public TempFile createTempFile(String filename_hint) throws Exception {
   403	            DefaultTempFile tempFile = new DefaultTempFile(this.tmpdir);
   404	            this.tempFiles.add(tempFile);
   405	            return tempFile;
   406	        }
   407	    }

### CMD: sed -n 494,520p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 494 (decodeHeader head)
   494	        private void decodeHeader(BufferedReader in, Map<String, String> pre, Map<String, String> parms, Map<String, String> headers) throws ResponseException {
   495	            try {
   496	                // Read the request line
   497	                String inLine = in.readLine();
   498	                if (inLine == null) {
   499	                    return;
   500	                }
   501	                StringTokenizer st = new StringTokenizer(inLine);
   502	                if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {
   503	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error. Usage: GET /example/file.html");
   504	                }
   505	                pre.put("method", st.nextToken());
   506	                if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {
   507	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Missing URI. Usage: GET /example/file.html");
   508	                }
   509	                String uri = st.nextToken();
   510	                // Decode parameters from the URI
   511	                int qmi = uri.indexOf('?');
   512	                if (qmi >= 0) {
   513	                    decodeParms(uri.substring(qmi + 1), parms);
   514	                    uri = decodePercent(uri.substring(0, qmi));
   515	                } else {
   516	                    uri = decodePercent(uri);
   517	                }
   518	                // If there's another token, its protocol version,
   519	                // followed by HTTP headers.
   520	                // NOTE: this now forces header names lower case since they are
### CMD: sed -n 655,760p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 655 (execute)
   655	        public void execute() throws IOException {
   656	            Response r = null;
   657	            try {
   658	                // Read the first 8192 bytes.
   659	                // The full header should fit in here.
   660	                // Apache's default header limit is 8KB.
   661	                // Do NOT assume that a single read will get the entire header
   662	                // at once!
   663	                byte[] buf = new byte[HTTPSession.BUFSIZE];
   664	                this.splitbyte = 0;
   665	                this.rlen = 0;
   666	                int read = -1;
   667	                this.inputStream.mark(HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
   668	                try {
   669	                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, 0, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
   670	                } catch (Exception e) {
   671	                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
   672	                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
   673	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
   674	                }
   675	                if (read == -1) {
   676	                    // socket was been closed
   677	                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
   678	                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
   679	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
   680	                }
   681	                while (read > 0) {
   682	                    this.rlen += read;
   683	                    this.splitbyte = findHeaderEnd(buf, this.rlen);
   684	                    if (this.splitbyte > 0) {
   685	                        break;
   686	                    }
   687	                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, this.rlen, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE - this.rlen);
   688	                }
   689	                if (this.splitbyte < this.rlen) {
   690	                    this.inputStream.reset();
   691	                    this.inputStream.skip(this.splitbyte);
   692	                }
   693	                this.parms = new HashMap<String, String>();
   694	                if (null == this.headers) {
   695	                    this.headers = new HashMap<String, String>();
   696	                } else {
   697	                    this.headers.clear();
   698	                }
   699	                // Create a BufferedReader for parsing the header.
   700	                BufferedReader hin = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(new ByteArrayInputStream(buf, 0, this.rlen)));
   701	                // Decode the header into parms and header java properties
   702	                Map<String, String> pre = new HashMap<String, String>();
   703	                decodeHeader(hin, pre, this.parms, this.headers);
   704	                if (null != this.remoteIp) {
   705	                    this.headers.put("remote-addr", this.remoteIp);
   706	                    this.headers.put("http-client-ip", this.remoteIp);
   707	                }
   708	                this.method = Method.lookup(pre.get("method"));
   709	                if (this.method == null) {
   710	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error.");
   711	                }
   712	                this.uri = pre.get("uri");
   713	                this.cookies = new CookieHandler(this.headers);
   714	                String connection = this.headers.get("connection");
   715	                boolean keepAlive = protocolVersion.equals("HTTP/1.1") && (connection == null || !connection.matches("(?i).*close.*"));
   716	                // Ok, now do the serve()
   717	                // TODO: long body_size = getBodySize();
   718	                // TODO: long pos_before_serve = this.inputStream.totalRead()
   719	                // (requires implementaion for totalRead())
   720	                r = serve(this);
   721	                // TODO: this.inputStream.skip(body_size -
   722	                // (this.inputStream.totalRead() - pos_before_serve))
   723	                if (r == null) {
   724	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: Serve() returned a null response.");
   725	                } else {
   726	                    String acceptEncoding = this.headers.get("accept-encoding");
   727	                    this.cookies.unloadQueue(r);
   728	                    r.setRequestMethod(this.method);
   729	                    r.setGzipEncoding(useGzipWhenAccepted(r) && acceptEncoding != null && acceptEncoding.contains("gzip"));
   730	                    r.setKeepAlive(keepAlive);
   731	                    r.send(this.outputStream);
   732	                }
   733	                if (!keepAlive || "close".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getHeader("connection"))) {
   734	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
   735	                }
   736	            } catch (SocketException e) {
   737	                // throw it out to close socket object (finalAccept)
   738	                throw e;
   739	            } catch (SocketTimeoutException ste) {
   740	                // treat socket timeouts the same way we treat socket exceptions
   741	                // i.e. close the stream & finalAccept object by throwing the
   742	                // exception up the call stack.
   743	                throw ste;
   744	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
   745	                Response resp = newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage());
   746	                resp.send(this.outputStream);
   747	                safeClose(this.outputStream);
   748	            } catch (ResponseException re) {
   749	                Response resp = newFixedLengthResponse(re.getStatus(), NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, re.getMessage());
   750	                resp.send(this.outputStream);
   751	                safeClose(this.outputStream);
   752	            } finally {
   753	                safeClose(r);
   754	                this.tempFileManager.clear();
   755	            }
   756	        }
   757	        /**
   758	         * Find byte index separating header from body. It must be the last byte
   759	         * of the first two sequential new lines.
   760	         */

   845	                throw new Error(e); // we won't recover, so throw an error
   846	            }
   847	        }

   853	         * Deduce body length in bytes. Either from "content-length" header or
   854	         * read bytes.
   855	         */
   856	        public long getBodySize() {
   857	            if (this.headers.containsKey("content-length")) {
   858	                return Long.parseLong(this.headers.get("content-length"));
   859	            } else if (this.splitbyte < this.rlen) {
   860	                return this.rlen - this.splitbyte;
### CMD: sed -n 150,200p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 150 (ClientHandler)
   150	        void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler);
   151	        void exec(ClientHandler code);
   152	    }
   153	    /**
   154	     * The runnable that will be used for every new client connection.
   155	     */
   156	    public class ClientHandler implements Runnable {
   157	        private final InputStream inputStream;
   158	        private final Socket acceptSocket;
   159	        private ClientHandler(InputStream inputStream, Socket acceptSocket) {
   160	            this.inputStream = inputStream;
   161	            this.acceptSocket = acceptSocket;
   162	        }
   163	        public void close() {
   164	            safeClose(this.inputStream);
   165	            safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
   166	        }
   167	        @Override
   168	        public void run() {
   169	            OutputStream outputStream = null;
   170	            try {
   171	                outputStream = this.acceptSocket.getOutputStream();
   172	                TempFileManager tempFileManager = NanoHTTPD.this.tempFileManagerFactory.create();
   173	                HTTPSession session = new HTTPSession(tempFileManager, this.inputStream, outputStream, this.acceptSocket.getInetAddress());
   174	                while (!this.acceptSocket.isClosed()) {
   175	                    session.execute();
   176	                }
   177	            } catch (Exception e) {
   178	                // When the socket is closed by the client,
   179	                // we throw our own SocketException
   180	                // to break the "keep alive" loop above. If
   181	                // the exception was anything other
   182	                // than the expected SocketException OR a
   183	                // SocketTimeoutException, print the
   184	                // stacktrace
   185	                if (!(e instanceof SocketException && "NanoHttpd Shutdown".equals(e.getMessage())) && !(e instanceof SocketTimeoutException)) {
   186	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
   187	                }
   188	            } finally {
   189	                safeClose(outputStream);
   190	                safeClose(this.inputStream);
   191	                safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
   192	                NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.closed(this);
   193	            }
   194	        }
   195	    }

### CMD: sed -n 1320,1356p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 1320 (ServerRunnable)
  1320	    }
  1321	    /**
  1322	     * The runnable that will be used for the main listening thread.
  1323	     */
  1324	    public class ServerRunnable implements Runnable {
  1325	        private final int timeout;
  1326	        private IOException bindException;
  1327	        private boolean hasBinded = false;
  1328	        private ServerRunnable(int timeout) {
  1329	            this.timeout = timeout;
  1330	        }
  1331	        @Override
  1332	        public void run() {
  1333	            try {
  1334	                myServerSocket.bind(hostname != null ? new InetSocketAddress(hostname, myPort) : new InetSocketAddress(myPort));
  1335	                hasBinded = true;
  1336	            } catch (IOException e) {
  1337	                this.bindException = e;
  1338	                return;
  1339	            }
  1340	            do {
  1341	                try {
  1342	                    final Socket finalAccept = NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.accept();
  1343	                    if (this.timeout > 0) {
  1344	                        finalAccept.setSoTimeout(this.timeout);
  1345	                    }
  1346	                    final InputStream inputStream = finalAccept.getInputStream();
  1347	                    NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.exec(createClientHandler(finalAccept, inputStream));
  1348	                } catch (IOException e) {
  1349	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
  1350	                }
  1351	            } while (!NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.isClosed());
  1352	        }
  1353	    }
  1354	    /**
  1355	     * A temp file.
  1356	     * <p/>
### CMD: sed -n 1505,1530p NanoHTTPD.java | nl -ba -v 1505 (safeClose)
  1505	        String mime = null;
  1506	        if (dot >= 0) {
  1507	            mime = mimeTypes().get(uri.substring(dot + 1).toLowerCase());
  1508	        }
  1509	        return mime == null ? "application/octet-stream" : mime;
  1510	    }
  1511	    private static final void safeClose(Object closeable) {
  1512	        try {
  1513	            if (closeable != null) {
  1514	                if (closeable instanceof Closeable) {
  1515	                    ((Closeable) closeable).close();
  1516	                } else if (closeable instanceof Socket) {
  1517	                    ((Socket) closeable).close();
  1518	                } else if (closeable instanceof ServerSocket) {
  1519	                    ((ServerSocket) closeable).close();
  1520	                } else {
  1521	                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown object to close");
  1522	                }
  1523	            }
  1524	        } catch (IOException e) {
  1525	            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not close", e);
  1526	        }
  1527	    }

  1802	    }
  1803	    /**
  1804	     * Start the server.
  1805	     *
  1806	     * @param timeout
  1807	     *            timeout to use for socket connections.
  1808	     * @param daemon
  1809	     *            start the thread daemon or not.
  1810	     * @throws IOException
  1811	     *             if the socket is in use.
  1812	     */
  1813	    public void start(final int timeout, boolean daemon) throws IOException {
  1814	        this.myServerSocket = this.getServerSocketFactory().create();
  1815	        this.myServerSocket.setReuseAddress(true);
  1816	        ServerRunnable serverRunnable = createServerRunnable(timeout);
  1817	        this.myThread = new Thread(serverRunnable);
  1818	        this.myThread.setDaemon(daemon);
  1819	        this.myThread.setName("NanoHttpd Main Listener");
  1820	        this.myThread.start();
  1821	        while (!serverRunnable.hasBinded && serverRunnable.bindException == null) {
  1822	            try {
  1823	                Thread.sleep(10L);
  1824	            } catch (Throwable e) {
  1825	                // on android this may not be allowed, that's why we
  1826	                // catch throwable the wait should be very short because we are
  1827	                // just waiting for the bind of the socket
  1828	            }
  1829	        }
  1830	        if (serverRunnable.bindException != null) {
  1831	            throw serverRunnable.bindException;
  1832	        }
  1833	    }
  1834	    /**
  1835	     * Stop the server.
  1836	     */
  1837	    public void stop() {
  1838	        try {
  1839	            safeClose(this.myServerSocket);
  1840	            this.asyncRunner.closeAll();
  1841	            if (this.myThread != null) {
  1842	                this.myThread.join();
  1843	            }
  1844	        } catch (Exception e) {
  1845	            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not stop all connections", e);
  1846	        }
  1847	    }
  1848	    public final boolean wasStarted() {
  1849	        return this.myServerSocket != null && this.myThread != null;
  1850	    }

  1732	    public Response serve(IHTTPSession session) {
  1733	        Map<String, String> files = new HashMap<String, String>();
  1734	        Method method = session.getMethod();
  1735	        if (Method.PUT.equals(method) || Method.POST.equals(method)) {
  1736	            try {
  1737	                session.parseBody(files);
  1738	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
  1739	                return newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage());
  1740	            } catch (ResponseException re) {
  1741	                return newFixedLengthResponse(re.getStatus(), NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, re.getMessage());
  1742	            }
  1743	        }
  1744	        Map<String, String> parms = session.getParms();
  1745	        parms.put(NanoHTTPD.QUERY_STRING_PARAMETER, session.getQueryParameterString());
  1746	        return serve(session.getUri(), method, session.getHeaders(), parms, files);
  1747	    }

   202	    public Flowable<Boolean> observeEngineRunning() {
   203	        return Flowable.create((emitter) -> {
   204	            if (emitter.isCancelled())
   205	                return;
   206	
   207	            TorrentEngineListener listener = new TorrentEngineListener() {
   208	                @Override
   209	                public void onSessionStarted() {
   210	                    if (!emitter.isCancelled())
   211	                        emitter.onNext(true);
   212	                }
   213	
   214	                @Override
   215	                public void onSessionStopped() {
   216	                    if (!emitter.isCancelled())
   217	                        emitter.onNext(false);
   218	                }
   219	            };
   220	
   221	            if (!emitter.isCancelled()) {
   222	                emitter.onNext(isRunning());
   223	                addListener(listener);
   224	                emitter.setDisposable(Disposables.fromAction(() -> removeListener(listener)));
   225	            }
   226	
   227	        }, BackpressureStrategy.LATEST);
   228	    }

   231	     * Only calls from TorrentService
   232	     */

   235	        if (isRunning())
   236	            return;
   237	
   238	        switchConnectionReceiver();
   239	        switchPowerReceiver();
   240	        disposables.add(pref.observeSettingsChanged()
   241	                .subscribe(this::handleSettingsChanged));
   242	
   243	        disposables.add(downloadsCompleted.listen()
   244	                .subscribe(
   245	                        this::handleAutoStop,
   246	                        (err) -> {
   247	                            Log.e(TAG, "Auto stop error: " +
   248	                                    Log.getStackTraceString(err));
   249	                            handleAutoStop();
   250	                        }
   251	                ));
   252	
   253	        disposables.add(session.getLogger().observeDataSetChanged()
   254	                .subscribe((change) -> {
   255	                    if (change.reason == Logger.DataSetChange.Reason.NEW_ENTRIES && change.entries != null)
   256	                        printSessionLog(change.entries);
   257	                }));
   258	
   259	        session.start();
   260	    }

   175	    @Override
   176	    public int onStartCommand(Intent intent, int flags, int startId)
   177	    {
   178	        String action = null;
   179	        if (intent != null)
   180	            action = intent.getAction();
   181	
   182	        /* Handle shutdown actions before init service */
   183	        if (action != null) {
   184	            int ret = handleShutdownActions(action);
   185	            if (ret >= 0)
   186	                return ret;
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        /* The first start */
   190	        if (isAlreadyRunning.compareAndSet(false, true))
   191	            init();
   192	
   193	        if (action != null)
   194	            handleActions(action);
   195	
   196	        return START_STICKY;
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    private int handleShutdownActions(String action)
   200	    {
   201	        switch (action) {
   202	            case NotificationReceiver.NOTIFY_ACTION_SHUTDOWN_APP:
   203	            case ACTION_SHUTDOWN:
   204	                shutdown();
   205	                return START_NOT_STICKY;
   206	            case ACTION_RESTART_FOREGROUND_NOTIFICATION:
   207	                makeForegroundNotify();
   208	                return START_STICKY;
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        return -1;
   212	    }

   317	    def impl(cls, name, desc=None):
   318	        """Node that runs when method `name` is invoked on an instance of class `cls` (short name)."""
   319	        tbl = (auto_data or {}).get('impl', {}).get(cls, {}).get(name)
   320	        if not tbl:
   321	            raise KeyError('impl: %s has no method %s' % (cls, name))
   322	        if desc:
   323	            return tbl[desc]
   324	        if len(set(tbl.values())) != 1:
   325	            raise KeyError('impl: %s.%s is overloaded, pass desc: %s' % (cls, name, sorted(tbl)))
   326	        return next(iter(tbl.values()))

   112	def method_names(rows):
   113	    """Base node name per method; overloads get '(params)'; classes that share a simple name get a package prefix."""
   114	    pre = class_prefixes(rows)
   115	    shorts = [pre.get(r['class'].split('$')[0], '') + r['short'] for r in rows]
   116	    count = collections.Counter(shorts)
   117	    names, by_name = [], {}
   118	    for r, sh in zip(rows, shorts):
   119	        n = sh if count[sh] == 1 else '%s(%s)' % (sh, param_names(r['desc']))
   120	        k = 2
   121	        while n in by_name:   # same class, overloads whose parameter simple names coincide
   122	            n = '%s#%d' % (n.split('#')[0], k)
   123	            k += 1
   124	        names.append(n)
   125	        by_name[n] = r
   126	    return names, by_name
   127

### CMD: sed -n 161,200p TorrentEngine.java (observeNeedStartEngine body)
   161	    public Flowable<Boolean> observeNeedStartEngine() {
   162	        return Flowable.create((emitter) -> {
   163	            if (emitter.isCancelled())
   164	                return;
   165	
   166	            Runnable emitLoop = () -> {
   167	                while (!Thread.interrupted()) {
   168	                    try {
   169	                        Thread.sleep(1000);
   170	
   171	                    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
   172	                        return;
   173	                    }
   174	
   175	                    if (emitter.isCancelled() || isRunning())
   176	                        return;
   177	
   178	                    emitter.onNext(true);
   179	                }
   180	            };
   181	
   182	            Disposable d = observeEngineRunning()
   183	                    .subscribeOn(Schedulers.io())
   184	                    .subscribe((isRunning) -> {
   185	                        if (emitter.isCancelled())
   186	                            return;
   187	
   188	                        if (!isRunning) {
   189	                            emitter.onNext(true);
   190	                            exec.submit(emitLoop);
   191	                        }
   192	                    });
   193	
   194	            if (!emitter.isCancelled()) {
   195	                emitter.onNext(!isRunning());
   196	                emitter.setDisposable(d);
   197	            }
   198	
   199	        }, BackpressureStrategy.LATEST);
   200	    }

   136	        String torrentId = Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("torrent"));
   137	
   138	        int fileIndex;
   139	        TorrentStream stream;
   140	        try {
   141	            fileIndex = Integer.parseInt(Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("file")));
   142	            stream = engine.getStream(torrentId, fileIndex);
   143	            if (stream == null)
   144	                return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
   145	
   146	        } catch (Exception e) {
   147	            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
   148	        }
   149	
   150	        Map<String, String> header = httpSession.getHeaders();
   151	        try {
   152	            Response res;
   153	            String etag = stream.id;
   154	            long startFrom = 0;
   155	            long endAt = -1;
   156	            String range = header.get("range");
   157	
   158	            if (range != null) {
   159	                if (range.startsWith("bytes=")) {
   160	                    range = range.substring("bytes=".length());
   161	                    int minus = range.indexOf('-');
   162	                    try {
   163	                        if (minus > 0) {
   164	                            startFrom = Long.parseLong(range.substring(0, minus));
   165	                            endAt = Long.parseLong(range.substring(minus + 1));
   166	                        }
   167	                    } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
   168	
   169	                    }
   170	                }
   171	            }
   172	
   173	            /*
   174	             * Get if-range header. If present, it must match etag or else we
   175	             * should ignore the range request
   176	             */
   177	            String ifRange = header.get("if-range");
   178	            boolean headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching = (ifRange == null || etag.equals(ifRange));
   179	
   180	            String ifNoneMatch = header.get("if-none-match");
   181	            boolean headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching = ifNoneMatch != null &&
   182	                    ("*".equals(ifNoneMatch) || ifNoneMatch.equals(etag));
   183	
   184	            if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null &&
   185	                startFrom >= 0 && startFrom < stream.fileSize) {
   186	                /*
   187	                 * Range request that matches current etag
   188	                 * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
   189	                 */
   190	                if (headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
   191	                    /*
   192	                     * Range request that matches current etag
   193	                     * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
   194	                     * would return range from file respond with not-modified
   195	                     */
   196	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
   197	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   198	
   199	                } else {
   200	                    if (endAt < 0)
   201	                        endAt = stream.fileSize - 1;
   202	                    long newLen = endAt - startFrom + 1;
   203	                    if (newLen < 0)
   204	                        newLen = 0;
   205	
   206	                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
   207	                    is.skip(startFrom);
   208	
   209	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(PARTIAL_CONTENT, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, newLen);
   210	                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
   211	                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + newLen);
   212	                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes " + startFrom + "-" + endAt + "/" + stream.fileSize);
   213	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   214	                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
   215	                }
   216	            } else {
   217	                if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null && startFrom >= stream.fileSize) {
   218	                    /*
   219	                     * Return the size of the file
   220	                     * 4xx responses are not trumped by if-none-match
   221	                     */
   222	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "");
   223	                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes */" + stream.fileSize);
   224	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   225	
   226	                } else if (range == null && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
   227	                    /*
   228	                     * Full-file-fetch request would return entire file
   229	                     * respond with not-modified
   230	                     */
   231	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
   232	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   233	
   234	                } else if (!headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
   235	                    /*
   236	                     * Range request that doesn't match current etag
   237	                     * would return entire (different) file respond with not-modified
   238	                     */
   239	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
   240	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   241	
   242	                } else {
   243	                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
   244	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(OK, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, stream.fileSize);
   245	                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
   246	                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + stream.fileSize);
   247	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   248	                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
   249	                }
   250	            }
   251	
   252	            return res;
   253	        } catch (Throwable e) {
   254	            Log.e(TAG, Log.getStackTraceString(e));
   255	
   256	            return newFixedLengthResponse(FORBIDDEN, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "Forbidden");
   257	        }

### CMD: sed -n 1,60p tools/build_graph.py (docstring, kinds, evidence)
     1	"""Build, validate and export a trigger graph from an app's graph_def.py (output schema 2, see handoff/SCHEMA_V2.md).
     2	
     3	Usage: python3 -I build_graph.py <graph_dir> <file_prefix>
     4	
     5	<graph_dir> must contain:
     6	  graph_def.py          edge definitions (see below)
     7	  method_list.json      output of list_methods.py --json (the method universe)
     8	  verification.csv      optional audit log: type,item,result,confirmed_by,ref,edges,note (see SCHEMA_V2.md)
     9	  coverage_baseline.json  optional; used only for the one-way sanity check (a method a tool ran cannot be UNREACHABLE)
    10	
    11	graph_def.py is executed with these names predefined:
    12	  e(src, dst, kind, trigger, gui=(), guard=None, reads=(), writes=(), note=None, needs_state=None, evidence=None,
    13	    env=None, cls=None, via=None, env_kinds=(), state_kinds=())      -> appends one edge
    14	  SCREENS = {}  TITLE = ''  NOTES = {}
    15	  UNREACHABLE = {method: (kind, reason[, source_ref[, confirmed_by]])}   kind in UNREACHABLE_KINDS
    16	  DEAD = {}  FRAMEWORK_ONLY = {}     legacy: {method: reason}; read as UNREACHABLE with kind 'unclassified'
    17	  ENV_KINDS_OF = {env text: [kinds]}  STATE_KINDS_OF = {needs_state text: [kinds]}   kinds of the gates, by exact text
    18	  CLASSIFY = None       optional callable(edge) -> edge class or (edge class, via); applied to edges without cls
    19	  EDIT = None           optional callable(edge): may change any field of every edge (e.g. add an ENV gate) before ids are made
    20	  STRICT = False        True: anything still unclassified is an error (set it once an app is converted)
    21	  HTML = True           write the viewer page if the template exists
    22	  PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV = False   # True: a method that needs in-app state but no environment gate is preferred
    23	                                  # over a route that needs the environment
    24	`env` marks an edge that needs the device or the outside world; `needs_state` an edge that needs earlier in-app actions.
    25	NOTES maps a base method name to a free-text note for methods.csv.
    26	USE_AUTO = True adds the call edges from <graph_dir>/auto_calls.json (tools/extract_calls.py) for every
    27	caller/callee pair that no hand-written edge already covers; SUPPRESS = {(src, dst), ...} or {src, ...}
    28	drops auto edges (e.g. calls inside branches that can never run). Lambda creations become REGISTER edges.
    29	
    30	Writes into <graph_dir>/out/:
    31	  <prefix>_trigger_graph.json, methods.csv, <prefix>_trigger_graph.html (if the template exists),
    32	  sanity_check.csv (tool coverage vs labels, never used to set a label), build_report.txt.
    33	methods.csv has route_edge_ids: the edge ids of the derived best route (what a verification.csv path row must repeat).
    34	"""
    35	import collections
    36	import csv
    37	import hashlib
    38	import json
    39	import os
    40	import re
    41	import sys
    42	
    43	KINDS = {'ENTRY', 'LIFECYCLE', 'GUI', 'REGISTER', 'CALL', 'INTENT', 'RESULT', 'ASYNC',
    44	         'FRAMEWORK', 'SYSTEM', 'ENTERS'}
    45	EVIDENCE = ('SRC', 'FW-SRC', 'DYN', 'FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED', 'UNVERIFIED')
    46	JVM = {'I': 'int', 'J': 'long', 'Z': 'boolean', 'F': 'float', 'D': 'double', 'B': 'byte',
    47	       'C': 'char', 'S': 'short', 'V': 'void'}
    48	TEMPLATE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'viewer_template.html')
    49	
    50	# schema 2 vocabularies
    51	EDGE_CLASSES = ('explicit', 'standard', 'non_standard', 'page')
    52	DEFAULT_CLASS = {'CALL': 'explicit', 'ENTRY': 'standard', 'LIFECYCLE': 'standard', 'GUI': 'standard', 'REGISTER': 'standard',
    53	                 'INTENT': 'standard', 'RESULT': 'standard', 'ASYNC': 'standard', 'FRAMEWORK': 'standard', 'SYSTEM': 'standard'}
    54	ENV_KINDS = ('network', 'tls_chain', 'permission', 'hardware', 'other_app', 'system_event', 'time', 'other')
    55	STATE_KINDS = ('data', 'settings', 'account', 'file', 'session')
    56	UNREACHABLE_KINDS = ('no_trigger_in_app', 'branch_never_taken', 'framework_never_calls', 'closed_by_configuration', 'closed_by_api_level')
    57	CONFIRMED = ('DEVICE', 'SOURCE', 'INFERRED')
    58	RESULTS = ('correct', 'fixed', 'wrong', 'unclear')
    59	VERIFY_TYPES = ('label', 'edge', 'path')
    60	# words that suggest a non-standard implicit edge (library or reflection makes the link); only used to print a review list
### CMD: sed -n 74,93p tools/build_graph.py (param_names)
    74	def param_names(desc):
    75	    """'(Landroid/content/Context;I)Z' -> 'Context,int'"""
    76	    args, i, out = desc[1:desc.index(')')], 0, []
    77	    while i < len(args):
    78	        dims = 0
    79	        while args[i] == '[':
    80	            dims += 1
    81	            i += 1
    82	        if args[i] == 'L':
    83	            j = args.index(';', i)
    84	            name = args[i + 1:j].rsplit('/', 1)[-1].split('$')[-1]
    85	            i = j + 1
    86	        else:
    87	            name = JVM[args[i]]
    88	            i += 1
    89	        out.append(name + '[]' * dims)
    90	    return ','.join(out)

   340	     * Default strategy for creating and cleaning up temporary files.
   341	     * <p/>
   342	     * <p>
   343	     * By default, files are created by <code>File.createTempFile()</code> in
   344	     * the directory specified.
   345	     * </p>
   346	     */
   347	    public static class DefaultTempFile implements TempFile {
   348	        private final File file;
   349	        private final OutputStream fstream;
   350	        public DefaultTempFile(File tempdir) throws IOException {
   351	            this.file = File.createTempFile("NanoHTTPD-", "", tempdir);
   352	            this.fstream = new FileOutputStream(this.file);
   353	        }
   354	        @Override
   355	        public void delete() throws Exception {
   356	            safeClose(this.fstream);
   357	            if (!this.file.delete()) {
   358	                throw new Exception("could not delete temporary file");
   359	            }
   360	        }
   361	        @Override
   362	        public String getName() {
   363	            return this.file.getAbsolutePath();
   364	        }
   365	        @Override
   366	        public OutputStream open() throws Exception {
   367	            return this.fstream;
   368	        }
   369	    }

   317	    def impl(cls, name, desc=None):
   318	        """Node that runs when method `name` is invoked on an instance of class `cls` (short name)."""
   319	        tbl = (auto_data or {}).get('impl', {}).get(cls, {}).get(name)
   320	        if not tbl:
   321	            raise KeyError('impl: %s has no method %s' % (cls, name))
   322	        if desc:
   323	            return tbl[desc]
   324	        if len(set(tbl.values())) != 1:
   325	            raise KeyError('impl: %s.%s is overloaded, pass desc: %s' % (cls, name, sorted(tbl)))
   326	        return next(iter(tbl.values()))

### CMD: sed -n 1,60p build_graph.py
     1	"""Build, validate and export a trigger graph from an app's graph_def.py (output schema 2, see handoff/SCHEMA_V2.md).
     2	
     3	Usage: python3 -I build_graph.py <graph_dir> <file_prefix>
     4	
     5	<graph_dir> must contain:
     6	  graph_def.py          edge definitions (see below)
     7	  method_list.json      output of list_methods.py --json (the method universe)
     8	  verification.csv      optional audit log: type,item,result,confirmed_by,ref,edges,note (see SCHEMA_V2.md)
     9	  coverage_baseline.json  optional; used only for the one-way sanity check (a method a tool ran cannot be UNREACHABLE)
    10	
    11	graph_def.py is executed with these names predefined:
    12	  e(src, dst, kind, trigger, gui=(), guard=None, reads=(), writes=(), note=None, needs_state=None, evidence=None,
    13	    env=None, cls=None, via=None, env_kinds=(), state_kinds=())      -> appends one edge
    14	  SCREENS = {}  TITLE = ''  NOTES = {}
    15	  UNREACHABLE = {method: (kind, reason[, source_ref[, confirmed_by]])}   kind in UNREACHABLE_KINDS
    16	  DEAD = {}  FRAMEWORK_ONLY = {}     legacy: {method: reason}; read as UNREACHABLE with kind 'unclassified'
    17	  ENV_KINDS_OF = {env text: [kinds]}  STATE_KINDS_OF = {needs_state text: [kinds]}   kinds of the gates, by exact text
    18	  CLASSIFY = None       optional callable(edge) -> edge class or (edge class, via); applied to edges without cls
    19	  EDIT = None           optional callable(edge): may change any field of every edge (e.g. add an ENV gate) before ids are made
    20	  STRICT = False        True: anything still unclassified is an error (set it once an app is converted)
    21	  HTML = True           write the viewer page if the template exists
    22	  PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV = False   # True: a method that needs in-app state but no environment gate is preferred
    23	                                  # over a route that needs the environment
    24	`env` marks an edge that needs the device or the outside world; `needs_state` an edge that needs earlier in-app actions.
    25	NOTES maps a base method name to a free-text note for methods.csv.
    26	USE_AUTO = True adds the call edges from <graph_dir>/auto_calls.json (tools/extract_calls.py) for every
    27	caller/callee pair that no hand-written edge already covers; SUPPRESS = {(src, dst), ...} or {src, ...}
    28	drops auto edges (e.g. calls inside branches that can never run). Lambda creations become REGISTER edges.
    29	
    30	Writes into <graph_dir>/out/:
    31	  <prefix>_trigger_graph.json, methods.csv, <prefix>_trigger_graph.html (if the template exists),
    32	  sanity_check.csv (tool coverage vs labels, never used to set a label), build_report.txt.
    33	methods.csv has route_edge_ids: the edge ids of the derived best route (what a verification.csv path row must repeat).
    34	"""
    35	import collections
    36	import csv
    37	import hashlib
    38	import json
    39	import os
    40	import re
    41	import sys
    42	
    43	KINDS = {'ENTRY', 'LIFECYCLE', 'GUI', 'REGISTER', 'CALL', 'INTENT', 'RESULT', 'ASYNC',
    44	         'FRAMEWORK', 'SYSTEM', 'ENTERS'}
    45	EVIDENCE = ('SRC', 'FW-SRC', 'DYN', 'FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED', 'UNVERIFIED')
    46	JVM = {'I': 'int', 'J': 'long', 'Z': 'boolean', 'F': 'float', 'D': 'double', 'B': 'byte',
    47	       'C': 'char', 'S': 'short', 'V': 'void'}
    48	TEMPLATE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'viewer_template.html')
    49	
    50	# schema 2 vocabularies
    51	EDGE_CLASSES = ('explicit', 'standard', 'non_standard', 'page')
    52	DEFAULT_CLASS = {'CALL': 'explicit', 'ENTRY': 'standard', 'LIFECYCLE': 'standard', 'GUI': 'standard', 'REGISTER': 'standard',
    53	                 'INTENT': 'standard', 'RESULT': 'standard', 'ASYNC': 'standard', 'FRAMEWORK': 'standard', 'SYSTEM': 'standard'}
    54	ENV_KINDS = ('network', 'tls_chain', 'permission', 'hardware', 'other_app', 'system_event', 'time', 'other')
    55	STATE_KINDS = ('data', 'settings', 'account', 'file', 'session')
    56	UNREACHABLE_KINDS = ('no_trigger_in_app', 'branch_never_taken', 'framework_never_calls', 'closed_by_configuration', 'closed_by_api_level')
    57	CONFIRMED = ('DEVICE', 'SOURCE', 'INFERRED')
    58	RESULTS = ('correct', 'fixed', 'wrong', 'unclear')
    59	VERIFY_TYPES = ('label', 'edge', 'path')
    60	# words that suggest a non-standard implicit edge (library or reflection makes the link); only used to print a review list
### CMD: sed -n 112,126p build_graph.py (method_names)
   112	def method_names(rows):
   113	    """Base node name per method; overloads get '(params)'; classes that share a simple name get a package prefix."""
   114	    pre = class_prefixes(rows)
   115	    shorts = [pre.get(r['class'].split('$')[0], '') + r['short'] for r in rows]
   116	    count = collections.Counter(shorts)
   117	    names, by_name = [], {}
   118	    for r, sh in zip(rows, shorts):
   119	        n = sh if count[sh] == 1 else '%s(%s)' % (sh, param_names(r['desc']))
   120	        k = 2
   121	        while n in by_name:   # same class, overloads whose parameter simple names coincide
   122	            n = '%s#%d' % (n.split('#')[0], k)
   123	            k += 1
   124	        names.append(n)
   125	        by_name[n] = r
   126	    return names, by_name

   377	            else:

   383	        o0 = collections.defaultdict(list)

   388	        if gone:

   390	            for s_ in gone:

   395	    classify = ns.get('CLASSIFY')

   397	    hint_review = []

   414	                                                   ('needs_state', 'state_kinds', state_of, STATE_KINDS, 'STATE')):
   415	            if not x[key]:
   416	                if x[kinds_key]:
   417	                    errors.append('%s: %s kinds without a %s gate text' % (x['id'], what, key))
   418	                continue

   422	                todo.append('%s: %s gate has no kinds: %s' % (x['id'], what, x[key][:80]))

   425	            if bad:

   427	            x[kinds_key] = kinds

   429	        errors.append('STRICT: AUTO_DEAD is not allowed (every UNREACHABLE needs an authored reason)')

   161	    public Flowable<Boolean> observeNeedStartEngine() {
   162	        return Flowable.create((emitter) -> {
   163	            if (emitter.isCancelled())
   164	                return;
   165	
   166	            Runnable emitLoop = () -> {
   167	                while (!Thread.interrupted()) {
   168	                    try {
   169	                        Thread.sleep(1000);
   170	
   171	                    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
   172	                        return;
   173	                    }
   174	
   175	                    if (emitter.isCancelled() || isRunning())
   176	                        return;
   177	
   178	                    emitter.onNext(true);
   179	                }
   180	            };
   181	
   182	            Disposable d = observeEngineRunning()
   183	                    .subscribeOn(Schedulers.io())
   184	                    .subscribe((isRunning) -> {
   185	                        if (emitter.isCancelled())
   186	                            return;
   187	
   188	                        if (!isRunning) {
   189	                            emitter.onNext(true);
   190	                            exec.submit(emitLoop);
   191	                        }
   192	                    });
   193	
   194	            if (!emitter.isCancelled()) {
   195	                emitter.onNext(!isRunning());
   196	                emitter.setDisposable(d);
   197	            }
   198	
   199	        }, BackpressureStrategy.LATEST);
   200	    }

   202	    public Flowable<Boolean> observeEngineRunning() {
   203	        return Flowable.create((emitter) -> {
   204	            if (emitter.isCancelled())
   205	                return;
   206	
   207	            TorrentEngineListener listener = new TorrentEngineListener() {
   208	                @Override
   209	                public void onSessionStarted() {
   210	                    if (!emitter.isCancelled())
   211	                        emitter.onNext(true);
   212	                }
   213	
   214	                @Override
   215	                public void onSessionStopped() {
   216	                    if (!emitter.isCancelled())
   217	                        emitter.onNext(false);
   218	                }
   219	            };
   220	
   221	            if (!emitter.isCancelled()) {
   222	                emitter.onNext(isRunning());
   223	                addListener(listener);
   224	                emitter.setDisposable(Disposables.fromAction(() -> removeListener(listener)));
   225	            }
   226	
   227	        }, BackpressureStrategy.LATEST);
   228	    }

   176	    public int onStartCommand(Intent intent, int flags, int startId)
   177	    {
   178	        String action = null;
   179	        if (intent != null)
   180	            action = intent.getAction();
   181	
   182	        /* Handle shutdown actions before init service */
   183	        if (action != null) {
   184	            int ret = handleShutdownActions(action);
   185	            if (ret >= 0)
   186	                return ret;
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        /* The first start */
   190	        if (isAlreadyRunning.compareAndSet(false, true))
   191	            init();
   192	
   193	        if (action != null)
   194	            handleActions(action);
   195	
   196	        return START_STICKY;
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    private int handleShutdownActions(String action)
   200	    {
   201	        switch (action) {
   202	            case NotificationReceiver.NOTIFY_ACTION_SHUTDOWN_APP:
   203	            case ACTION_SHUTDOWN:
   204	                shutdown();
   205	                return START_NOT_STICKY;
   206	            case ACTION_RESTART_FOREGROUND_NOTIFICATION:
   207	                makeForegroundNotify();
   208	                return START_STICKY;
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        return -1;
   212	    }

### CMD: nl NanoHTTPD.java 156-195 (ClientHandler) and 309-338 (DefaultAsyncRunner)
   156	    public class ClientHandler implements Runnable {
   157	        private final InputStream inputStream;
   158	        private final Socket acceptSocket;
   159	        private ClientHandler(InputStream inputStream, Socket acceptSocket) {
   160	            this.inputStream = inputStream;
   161	            this.acceptSocket = acceptSocket;
   162	        }
   163	        public void close() {
   164	            safeClose(this.inputStream);
   165	            safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
   166	        }
   167	        @Override
   168	        public void run() {
   169	            OutputStream outputStream = null;
   170	            try {
   171	                outputStream = this.acceptSocket.getOutputStream();
   172	                TempFileManager tempFileManager = NanoHTTPD.this.tempFileManagerFactory.create();
   173	                HTTPSession session = new HTTPSession(tempFileManager, this.inputStream, outputStream, this.acceptSocket.getInetAddress());
   174	                while (!this.acceptSocket.isClosed()) {
   175	                    session.execute();
   176	                }
   177	            } catch (Exception e) {
   178	                // When the socket is closed by the client,
   179	                // we throw our own SocketException
   180	                // to break the "keep alive" loop above. If
   181	                // the exception was anything other
   182	                // than the expected SocketException OR a
   183	                // SocketTimeoutException, print the
   184	                // stacktrace
   185	                if (!(e instanceof SocketException && "NanoHttpd Shutdown".equals(e.getMessage())) && !(e instanceof SocketTimeoutException)) {
   186	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
   187	                }
   188	            } finally {
   189	                safeClose(outputStream);
   190	                safeClose(this.inputStream);
   191	                safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
   192	                NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.closed(this);
   193	            }
   194	        }
   195	    }
   309	    public static class DefaultAsyncRunner implements AsyncRunner {
   310	        private long requestCount;
   311	        private final List<ClientHandler> running = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<NanoHTTPD.ClientHandler>());
   312	        /**
   313	         * @return a list with currently running clients.
   314	         */
   315	        public List<ClientHandler> getRunning() {
   316	            return running;
   317	        }
   318	        @Override
   319	        public void closeAll() {
   320	            // copy of the list for concurrency
   321	            for (ClientHandler clientHandler : new ArrayList<ClientHandler>(this.running)) {
   322	                clientHandler.close();
   323	            }
   324	        }
   325	        @Override
   326	        public void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
   327	            this.running.remove(clientHandler);
   328	        }
   329	        @Override
   330	        public void exec(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
   331	            ++this.requestCount;
   332	            Thread t = new Thread(clientHandler);
   333	            t.setDaemon(true);
   334	            t.setName("NanoHttpd Request Processor (#" + this.requestCount + ")");
   335	            this.running.add(clientHandler);
   336	            t.start();
   337	        }
   338	    }
### CMD: nl NanoHTTPD.java 380-400 (DefaultTempFileManager clear loop)
   380	    public static class DefaultTempFileManager implements TempFileManager {
   381	        private final File tmpdir;
   382	        private final List<TempFile> tempFiles;
   383	        public DefaultTempFileManager() {
   384	            this.tmpdir = new File(System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir"));
   385	            if (!tmpdir.exists()) {
   386	                tmpdir.mkdirs();
   387	            }
   388	            this.tempFiles = new ArrayList<TempFile>();
   389	        }
   390	        @Override
   391	        public void clear() {
   392	            for (TempFile file : this.tempFiles) {
   393	                try {
   394	                    file.delete();
   395	                } catch (Exception ignored) {
   396	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "could not delete file ", ignored);
   397	                }
   398	            }
   399	            this.tempFiles.clear();
   400	        }
### CMD: nl NanoHTTPD.java 655-760 (HTTPSession.execute)
   655	        public void execute() throws IOException {
   656	            Response r = null;
   657	            try {
   658	                // Read the first 8192 bytes.
   659	                // The full header should fit in here.
   660	                // Apache's default header limit is 8KB.
   661	                // Do NOT assume that a single read will get the entire header
   662	                // at once!
   663	                byte[] buf = new byte[HTTPSession.BUFSIZE];
   664	                this.splitbyte = 0;
   665	                this.rlen = 0;
   666	                int read = -1;
   667	                this.inputStream.mark(HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
   668	                try {
   669	                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, 0, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
   670	                } catch (Exception e) {
   671	                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
   672	                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
   673	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
   674	                }
   675	                if (read == -1) {
   676	                    // socket was been closed
   677	                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
   678	                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
   679	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
   680	                }
   681	                while (read > 0) {
   682	                    this.rlen += read;
   683	                    this.splitbyte = findHeaderEnd(buf, this.rlen);
   684	                    if (this.splitbyte > 0) {
   685	                        break;
   686	                    }
   687	                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, this.rlen, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE - this.rlen);
   688	                }
   689	                if (this.splitbyte < this.rlen) {
   690	                    this.inputStream.reset();
   691	                    this.inputStream.skip(this.splitbyte);
   692	                }
   693	                this.parms = new HashMap<String, String>();
   694	                if (null == this.headers) {
   695	                    this.headers = new HashMap<String, String>();
   696	                } else {
   697	                    this.headers.clear();
   698	                }
   699	                // Create a BufferedReader for parsing the header.
   700	                BufferedReader hin = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(new ByteArrayInputStream(buf, 0, this.rlen)));
   701	                // Decode the header into parms and header java properties
   702	                Map<String, String> pre = new HashMap<String, String>();
   703	                decodeHeader(hin, pre, this.parms, this.headers);
   704	                if (null != this.remoteIp) {
   705	                    this.headers.put("remote-addr", this.remoteIp);
   706	                    this.headers.put("http-client-ip", this.remoteIp);
   707	                }
   708	                this.method = Method.lookup(pre.get("method"));
   709	                if (this.method == null) {
   710	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error.");
   711	                }
   712	                this.uri = pre.get("uri");
   713	                this.cookies = new CookieHandler(this.headers);
   714	                String connection = this.headers.get("connection");
   715	                boolean keepAlive = protocolVersion.equals("HTTP/1.1") && (connection == null || !connection.matches("(?i).*close.*"));
   716	                // Ok, now do the serve()
   717	                // TODO: long body_size = getBodySize();
   718	                // TODO: long pos_before_serve = this.inputStream.totalRead()
   719	                // (requires implementaion for totalRead())
   720	                r = serve(this);
   721	                // TODO: this.inputStream.skip(body_size -
   722	                // (this.inputStream.totalRead() - pos_before_serve))
   723	                if (r == null) {
   724	                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: Serve() returned a null response.");
   725	                } else {
   726	                    String acceptEncoding = this.headers.get("accept-encoding");
   727	                    this.cookies.unloadQueue(r);
   728	                    r.setRequestMethod(this.method);
   729	                    r.setGzipEncoding(useGzipWhenAccepted(r) && acceptEncoding != null && acceptEncoding.contains("gzip"));
   730	                    r.setKeepAlive(keepAlive);
   731	                    r.send(this.outputStream);
   732	                }
   733	                if (!keepAlive || "close".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getHeader("connection"))) {
   734	                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
   735	                }
   736	            } catch (SocketException e) {
   737	                // throw it out to close socket object (finalAccept)
   738	                throw e;
   739	            } catch (SocketTimeoutException ste) {
   740	                // treat socket timeouts the same way we treat socket exceptions
   741	                // i.e. close the stream & finalAccept object by throwing the
   742	                // exception up the call stack.
   743	                throw ste;
   744	            } catch (IOException ioe) {
   745	                Response resp = newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage());
   746	                resp.send(this.outputStream);
   747	                safeClose(this.outputStream);
   748	            } catch (ResponseException re) {
   749	                Response resp = newFixedLengthResponse(re.getStatus(), NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, re.getMessage());
   750	                resp.send(this.outputStream);
   751	                safeClose(this.outputStream);
   752	            } finally {
   753	                safeClose(r);
   754	                this.tempFileManager.clear();
   755	            }
   756	        }
   757	        /**
   758	         * Find byte index separating header from body. It must be the last byte
   759	         * of the first two sequential new lines.
   760	         */

  1331	        @Override
  1332	        public void run() {
  1333	            try {
  1334	                myServerSocket.bind(hostname != null ? new InetSocketAddress(hostname, myPort) : new InetSocketAddress(myPort));
  1335	                hasBinded = true;
  1336	            } catch (IOException e) {
  1337	                this.bindException = e;

  1339	            }

  1341	                try {
  1342	                    final Socket finalAccept = NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.accept();
  1343	                    if (this.timeout > 0) {
  1344	                        finalAccept.setSoTimeout(this.timeout);
  1345	                    }
  1346	                    final InputStream inputStream = finalAccept.getInputStream();

  1348	                } catch (IOException e) {
  1349	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
  1350	                }
  1351	            } while (!NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.isClosed());

  1511	    private static final void safeClose(Object closeable) {
  1512	        try {
  1513	            if (closeable != null) {
  1514	                if (closeable instanceof Closeable) {
  1515	                    ((Closeable) closeable).close();
  1516	                } else if (closeable instanceof Socket) {
  1517	                    ((Socket) closeable).close();
  1518	                } else if (closeable instanceof ServerSocket) {
  1519	                    ((ServerSocket) closeable).close();
  1520	                } else {
  1521	                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown object to close");
  1522	                }
  1523	            }
  1524	        } catch (IOException e) {
  1525	            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not close", e);
  1526	        }
  1527	    }

   123	
   124	    public Response handleTorrent(IHTTPSession httpSession)
   125	    {
   126	        if (engine == null)
   127	            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
   128	
   129	        if (!httpSession.getUri().equals("/stream"))
   130	            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");
   131	
   132	        Map<String, String> params = httpSession.getParms();
   133	        if (params.size() < 2)
   134	            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");

### CMD: nl TorrentStreamServer.java 136-262 (handleTorrent)
   136	        String torrentId = Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("torrent"));
   137	
   138	        int fileIndex;
   139	        TorrentStream stream;
   140	        try {
   141	            fileIndex = Integer.parseInt(Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("file")));
   142	            stream = engine.getStream(torrentId, fileIndex);
   143	            if (stream == null)
   144	                return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
   145	
   146	        } catch (Exception e) {
   147	            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
   148	        }
   149	
   150	        Map<String, String> header = httpSession.getHeaders();
   151	        try {
   152	            Response res;
   153	            String etag = stream.id;
   154	            long startFrom = 0;
   155	            long endAt = -1;
   156	            String range = header.get("range");
   157	
   158	            if (range != null) {
   159	                if (range.startsWith("bytes=")) {
   160	                    range = range.substring("bytes=".length());
   161	                    int minus = range.indexOf('-');
   162	                    try {
   163	                        if (minus > 0) {
   164	                            startFrom = Long.parseLong(range.substring(0, minus));
   165	                            endAt = Long.parseLong(range.substring(minus + 1));
   166	                        }
   167	                    } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
   168	
   169	                    }
   170	                }
   171	            }
   172	
   173	            /*
   174	             * Get if-range header. If present, it must match etag or else we
   175	             * should ignore the range request
   176	             */
   177	            String ifRange = header.get("if-range");
   178	            boolean headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching = (ifRange == null || etag.equals(ifRange));
   179	
   180	            String ifNoneMatch = header.get("if-none-match");
   181	            boolean headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching = ifNoneMatch != null &&
   182	                    ("*".equals(ifNoneMatch) || ifNoneMatch.equals(etag));
   183	
   184	            if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null &&
   185	                startFrom >= 0 && startFrom < stream.fileSize) {
   186	                /*
   187	                 * Range request that matches current etag
   188	                 * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
   189	                 */
   190	                if (headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
   191	                    /*
   192	                     * Range request that matches current etag
   193	                     * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
   194	                     * would return range from file respond with not-modified
   195	                     */
   196	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
   197	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   198	
   199	                } else {
   200	                    if (endAt < 0)
   201	                        endAt = stream.fileSize - 1;
   202	                    long newLen = endAt - startFrom + 1;
   203	                    if (newLen < 0)
   204	                        newLen = 0;
   205	
   206	                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
   207	                    is.skip(startFrom);
   208	
   209	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(PARTIAL_CONTENT, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, newLen);
   210	                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
   211	                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + newLen);
   212	                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes " + startFrom + "-" + endAt + "/" + stream.fileSize);
   213	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   214	                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
   215	                }
   216	            } else {
   217	                if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null && startFrom >= stream.fileSize) {
   218	                    /*
   219	                     * Return the size of the file
   220	                     * 4xx responses are not trumped by if-none-match
   221	                     */
   222	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "");
   223	                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes */" + stream.fileSize);
   224	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   225	
   226	                } else if (range == null && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
   227	                    /*
   228	                     * Full-file-fetch request would return entire file
   229	                     * respond with not-modified
   230	                     */
   231	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
   232	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   233	
   234	                } else if (!headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
   235	                    /*
   236	                     * Range request that doesn't match current etag
   237	                     * would return entire (different) file respond with not-modified
   238	                     */
   239	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
   240	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   241	
   242	                } else {
   243	                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
   244	                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(OK, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, stream.fileSize);
   245	                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
   246	                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + stream.fileSize);
   247	                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
   248	                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
   249	                }
   250	            }
   251	
   252	            return res;
   253	        } catch (Throwable e) {
   254	            Log.e(TAG, Log.getStackTraceString(e));
   255	
   256	            return newFixedLengthResponse(FORBIDDEN, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "Forbidden");
   257	        }
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    static class DLNAFileType
   261	    {
   262	        public final String dlnaContentFeatures;

   226	     * features.

   228	     * @author LordFokas
